import hashlib  # noqa: E402
import json  # noqa: E402
import re  # noqa: E402
import shutil  # noqa: E402
import subprocess  # noqa: E402
import time  # noqa: E402

import numpy as np  # noqa: E402


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# timing helper

class Timer:
    """Collects wall-clock seconds per named stage: `with T("demucs"): ...`."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.t = {}

    def __call__(self, name):
        timer = self

        class _Ctx:
            def __enter__(self):
                self.t0 = time.perf_counter()
                print(f"[{name}] ...", flush=True)

            def __exit__(self, *exc):
                dt = time.perf_counter() - self.t0
                timer.t[name] = round(timer.t.get(name, 0.0) + dt, 2)
                print(f"[{name}] {dt:.1f}s", flush=True)
        return _Ctx()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# audio decoding

def mp3_gapless_info(path):
    """Read the Xing/Info + LAME tag of an mp3 (if any).

    Returns dict(encoder, enc_delay, enc_padding) or {} if no LAME tag.  ffmpeg's
    mp3 decoder (and Chrome/Firefox, which honour the same tag) trims
    enc_delay + 529 decoder-delay samples from the start and the padding from
    the end, so a decode with ffmpeg is the gapless "browser" timeline.  Without
    the tag nothing is trimmed anywhere, which is also consistent."""
    p = Path(path)
    if p.suffix.lower() != ".mp3":
        return {}
    b = p.read_bytes()[:1 << 16]
    off = 0
    if b[:3] == b"ID3":
        sz = b[6:10]
        off = 10 + ((sz[0] & 0x7F) << 21 | (sz[1] & 0x7F) << 14 | (sz[2] & 0x7F) << 7 | (sz[3] & 0x7F))
    # first frame sync
    while off + 4 < len(b) and not (b[off] == 0xFF and (b[off + 1] & 0xE0) == 0xE0):
        off += 1
    for tag in (b"Xing", b"Info"):
        x = b.find(tag, off, off + 200)
        if x < 0:
            continue
        flags = int.from_bytes(b[x + 4:x + 8], "big")
        q = x + 8 + (4 if flags & 1 else 0) + (4 if flags & 2 else 0) + (100 if flags & 4 else 0) + (4 if flags & 8 else 0)
        enc = b[q:q + 9]
        d = b[q + 21:q + 24]
        if len(d) < 3:
            return {}
        return dict(encoder=enc.decode("latin-1", "replace").strip("\x00 "),
                    enc_delay=(d[0] << 4) | (d[1] >> 4), enc_padding=((d[1] & 0x0F) << 8) | d[2])
    return {}


def decode(path, sr=SR, mono=False):
    """Gapless decode with ffmpeg -> float32 array, shape (n,) if mono else (2, n).

    This is THE timeline for every output: lyrics.json, audio.json, stems."""
    if shutil.which("ffmpeg") is None:
        raise SystemExit("ffmpeg not found on PATH")
    ch = 1 if mono else 2
    cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-nostdin", "-v", "error", "-i", str(path), "-vn", "-ac", str(ch), "-ar", str(sr),
           "-f", "f32le", "-acodec", "pcm_f32le", "-"]
    raw = subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True).stdout
    y = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype="<f4").copy()
    return y if mono else y.reshape(-1, 2).T.copy()


def audio_id(path):
    """Stable id for an input file (name + content hash) used for the work dir."""
    h = hashlib.sha1()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    stem = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_-]+", "_", Path(path).stem)[:40]
    return f"{stem}-{h.hexdigest()[:10]}"


def work_dir(path):
    d = WORK / audio_id(path)
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return d


def resample(y, sr_in, sr_out):
    if sr_in == sr_out:
        return y
    import soxr
    return soxr.resample(y, sr_in, sr_out).astype(np.float32)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# lyric sheet parsing (Suno format)

STAGE_DIRECTIONS = re.compile(
    r"^(instrumental|.*\bsolo|.*\bbreak(down)?|beat drop|drop|fade( out)?|silence|pause|applause|"
    r"music|outro music|intro music|end)$", re.I)


def parse_sheet(text):
    """Suno lyric sheet -> list of lines.

    `[Section - notes]` header lines set the section name (everything after
    " - " is stripped).  Blank lines are ignored.  Every other line is a lyric
    line; its tokens (split on whitespace) keep their display spelling.  Tokens
    inside (...) are backing vocals (bv=True); parentheticals that are only a
    stage direction like "(instrumental)" are dropped from the line.

    Returns [dict(i, text, section, words=[dict(w, bv)])]."""
    lines, section = [], ""
    for raw in text.splitlines():
        s = raw.strip()
        if not s:
            continue
        if s.startswith("[") and s.endswith("]"):
            section = s[1:-1].split(" - ")[0].strip()
            continue
        # drop pure stage-direction parentheticals
        s = re.sub(r"\(([^()]*)\)", lambda m: "" if STAGE_DIRECTIONS.match(m.group(1).strip(" .")) else m.group(0), s)
        s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s).strip()
        if not s:
            continue
        words, depth = [], 0
        for tok in s.split(" "):
            opens, closes = tok.count("("), tok.count(")")
            bv = depth > 0 or opens > 0
            depth = max(0, depth + opens - closes)
            words.append(dict(w=tok, bv=bv))
        if not any(units(w["w"]) for w in words):
            continue  # punctuation-only line
        lines.append(dict(i=len(lines), text=s, section=section, words=words))
    return lines


def load_sheet(path):
    return parse_sheet(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# text normalisation: display token -> pronounceable sub-word units
# (letters a-z and internal apostrophes).  Used for the CTC targets AND for
# matching Whisper's words, so both sides speak the same spelling.

LETTER_NAMES = dict(a="ay", b="bee", c="see", d="dee", e="ee", f="eff", g="gee", h="aitch", i="eye", j="jay",
                    k="kay", l="el", m="em", n="en", o="oh", p="pee", q="cue", r="are", s="ess", t="tee", u="you",
                    v="vee", w="double you", x="ex", y="why", z="zee")
_ONES = "zero one two three four five six seven eight nine ten eleven twelve thirteen fourteen fifteen " \
        "sixteen seventeen eighteen nineteen".split()
_TENS = "_ _ twenty thirty forty fifty sixty seventy eighty ninety".split()
_ORD = dict(one="first", two="second", three="third", five="fifth", eight="eighth", nine="ninth", twelve="twelfth")


def num_words(n, year_style=True):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 20:
        return _ONES[n]
    if n < 100:
        return _TENS[n // 10] + ("" if n % 10 == 0 else " " + _ONES[n % 10])
    if year_style and 1100 <= n <= 2099 and n % 100 != 0 and not (2000 <= n < 2010):
        return num_words(n // 100) + " " + (("oh " + _ONES[n % 100]) if n % 100 < 10 else num_words(n % 100))
    if n < 1000:
        return _ONES[n // 100] + " hundred" + ("" if n % 100 == 0 else " " + num_words(n % 100))
    if n < 1_000_000:
        return num_words(n // 1000, False) + " thousand" + ("" if n % 1000 == 0 else " " + num_words(n % 1000, False))
    return " ".join(_ONES[int(c)] for c in str(n))


def _ordinal(words):
    *head, last = words.split()
    if last in _ORD:
        last = _ORD[last]
    elif last.endswith("y"):
        last = last[:-1] + "ieth"
    else:
        last = last + "th"
    return " ".join(head + [last])


# words sung as a word even though they are written in capitals
CAPS_AS_WORD = {"OK": "okay", "I": "i", "A": "a", "TV": "tee vee"}
PRON_OVERRIDES = {}  # filled from --pron JSON: {"Piaget's": "pee ah zhays"}


def _expand(chunk):
    """One alphanumeric chunk (no spaces/hyphens) -> spoken text."""
    if not chunk:
        return ""
    m = re.fullmatch(r"(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(am|pm)?", chunk, re.I)
    if m:  # clock time 5:40, 10:45, 12:00pm
        h, mm, ap = int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2)), m.group(3)
        s = num_words(h) + (" o'clock" if mm == 0 and not ap else "" if mm == 0 else
                            " oh " + _ONES[mm] if mm < 10 else " " + num_words(mm))
        return s + (" " + " ".join(LETTER_NAMES[c] for c in ap.lower()) if ap else "")
    m = re.fullmatch(r"(\d+)(st|nd|rd|th)", chunk, re.I)
    if m:
        return _ordinal(num_words(m.group(1), False))
    m = re.fullmatch(r"(\d+)([a-z']*)", chunk, re.I)
    if m and m.group(2).lower() in ("am", "pm"):
        return num_words(m.group(1)) + " " + " ".join(LETTER_NAMES[c] for c in m.group(2).lower())
    if m and m.group(2).lower() in ("s", "'s"):  # 90s / 80's
        w = num_words(m.group(1))
        return (w[:-1] + "ies") if w.endswith("y") else w + "s"
    if m and m.group(2) == "":
        return num_words(m.group(1))
    if re.search(r"\d", chunk):  # mixed like 4k, mp3
        return " ".join(_expand(p) for p in re.findall(r"\d+|[A-Za-z']+", chunk))
    if chunk in CAPS_AS_WORD:
        return CAPS_AS_WORD[chunk]
    letters = chunk.replace("'", "")
    if len(letters) >= 2 and letters.isupper():
        has_vowel = re.search(r"[AEIOU]", letters) is not None
        if not has_vowel or len(letters) <= 3 and chunk not in ("BOO", "WOW", "YEAH", "OOH", "AAH", "OH", "AH", "NO",
                                                                  "YES", "DOG", "CAT", "BOY", "MAN", "GOD", "HEY",
                                                                  "OUT", "THE", "AND", "YOU", "ALL", "BED", "UP"):
            # acronym, sung letter by letter (BPM, AI, DJ)
            spelled = " ".join(LETTER_NAMES[c] for c in letters.lower())
            return spelled + ("s" if chunk.endswith("'s") else "")
    return chunk.lower()


def units(token):
    """Display token -> list of sub-word units for alignment.

    '2am' -> ['two', 'ay', 'em'];  '5:40' -> ['five', 'forty'];
    'hundred-twenty-eight' -> ['hundred', 'twenty', 'eight'];  'a.m.' -> ['ay', 'em'];
    'BPM' -> ['bee', 'pee', 'em'];  "(I'm" -> ["i'm"];  'untz.' -> ['untz']."""
    t = token.replace("’", "'").replace("‘", "'").replace("`", "'")
    if t in PRON_OVERRIDES:
        return PRON_OVERRIDES[t].split()
    core = t.strip("()[]{}\"“”,.;:!?…*_~-—–")
    if core in PRON_OVERRIDES:
        return PRON_OVERRIDES[core].split()
    # a.m. / p.m. / U.S.
    if re.fullmatch(r"([A-Za-z]\.){2,}", t.strip("()\"“”,;:!?")):
        return [LETTER_NAMES[c] for c in re.sub(r"[^a-z]", "", t.lower())]
    t = t.replace("&", " and ").replace("%", " percent").replace("+", " plus ").replace("@", " at ")
    out = []
    for chunk in re.split(r"[\s\-—–/]+", t):
        # keep clock times, strip other punctuation (but not internal apostrophes)
        chunk = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9':]", "", chunk)
        chunk = re.sub(r":(?!\d)", "", chunk).strip(":")
        chunk = chunk.strip("'")
        if not chunk:
            continue
        for w in _expand(chunk).split():
            w = re.sub(r"[^a-z']", "", w.lower()).strip("'")
            if w:
                out.append(w)
    return out


def norm_word(w):
    """Comparison key for fuzzy matching (apostrophes dropped)."""
    return w.replace("'", "")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# syllables (orthographic; only used to split a word's CTC span into "syl")

_DIGRAPHS = ("tch", "sch", "ch", "sh", "th", "ph", "wh", "ck", "ng", "gh", "qu", "gu")
_ONSETS = {"bl", "br", "cl", "cr", "dr", "fl", "fr", "gl", "gr", "pl", "pr", "sc", "sk", "sl", "sm", "sn", "sp",
           "st", "sw", "tr", "tw", "thr", "str", "spr", "scr", "shr", "squ", "sch", "kn", "wr", "chr", "phr"}
_pyphen = None


def _nuclei(w):
    """Vowel-nucleus spans [(a, b)] of a lowercase word (y/w handled as glides)."""
    V = set("aeiou")
    isv = []
    for k, c in enumerate(w):
        prev_v = k > 0 and isv[k - 1]
        nxt = w[k + 1] if k + 1 < len(w) else ""
        if c in V:
            v = not (c == "u" and k > 0 and w[k - 1] == "q")
        elif c == "y":
            v = k > 0 and (not prev_v or nxt not in V)
        elif c == "w":
            v = prev_v and nxt not in V
        else:
            v = False
        isv.append(v)
    spans, k = [], 0
    while k < len(w):
        if isv[k]:
            a = k
            while k < len(w) and isv[k]:
                k += 1
            spans.append([a, k])
        else:
            k += 1
    # hiatus splits: cry-ing, pi-a, re-al, cre-ate ... (vowel pairs that are two syllables)
    out = []
    for a, b in spans:
        g = w[a:b]
        cut = None
        for pat in ("yi", "ia", "io", "iu", "eo", "ua", "uo", "ya", "yo", "ee-i", "ea-t"):
            p = pat.split("-")[0]
            j = g.find(p)
            if j >= 0 and p in ("yi", "ia", "io", "iu", "eo", "ua", "uo", "ya", "yo") and not (
                    p == "io" and w[a + j + 2:a + j + 3] == "n" and a + j > 0 and w[a + j - 1] in "tsc"):  # -tion
                cut = a + j + 1
                break
        if cut:
            out += [[a, cut], [cut, b]]
        else:
            out.append([a, b])
    spans = out
    # silent final e / es / ed
    if len(spans) >= 2:
        a, b = spans[-1]
        tail = w[a:]
        pre = w[:a]
        if tail == "e" and not (len(pre) >= 2 and pre[-1] == "l" and pre[-2] not in "aeiouyl"):
            spans.pop()
        elif tail in ("es", "e's") and not re.search(r"(s|x|z|ch|sh|c|g)$", pre):
            spans.pop()
        elif tail == "ed" and not re.search(r"[td]$", pre):
            spans.pop()
    # suffix-silent e: extreme-ly, time-less, move-ment
    for suf in ("ly", "less", "ment", "ness", "ful"):
        if w.endswith(suf) and len(spans) >= 3:
            a, b = spans[-2]
            if w[a:b] == "e" and b == len(w) - len(suf) and spans[-2][0] > spans[-3][1]:
                spans.pop(-2)
            break
    return spans


def _heuristic_split(w):
    sp = _nuclei(w)
    if len(sp) <= 1:
        return [w]
    cuts = []
    for (a0, b0), (a1, b1) in zip(sp, sp[1:]):
        cl = w[b0:a1]
        if not cl:
            cuts.append(b0)
            continue
        # tokenise cluster into consonant units (digraphs kept)
        u, k = [], 0
        while k < len(cl):
            for d in _DIGRAPHS:
                if cl.startswith(d, k):
                    u.append(d)
                    k += len(d)
                    break
            else:
                u.append(cl[k])
                k += 1
        if len(u) == 1:
            cuts.append(b0 if u[0] != "x" else b0 + 1)
            continue
        # longest valid onset suffix, leaving >= 1 unit in the coda
        best = len(u) - 1
        for j in range(1, len(u)):
            if "".join(u[j:]) in _ONSETS:
                best = j
                break
        cuts.append(b0 + len("".join(u[:best])))
    pieces, last = [], 0
    for c in cuts:
        pieces.append(w[last:c])
        last = c
    pieces.append(w[last:])
    return [p for p in pieces if p]


def syllables(unit):
    """Split one alignment unit (lowercase letters/apostrophes) into syllable
    strings whose concatenation is the unit: 'crying' -> ['cry', 'ing']."""
    global _pyphen
    w = unit
    if len(w) <= 3:
        return [w]
    if _pyphen is None:
        try:
            import pyphen
            _pyphen = pyphen.Pyphen(lang="en_US")
        except Exception:
            _pyphen = False
    n_heur = len(_nuclei(w))
    if n_heur <= 1:
        return [w]
    pieces = _pyphen.inserted(w).split("-") if _pyphen else [w]
    # merge vowel-less pieces into neighbours
    merged = []
    for p in pieces:
        if merged and not re.search(r"[aeiouy]", p):
            merged[-1] += p
        elif merged and not re.search(r"[aeiouy]", merged[-1]):
            merged[-1] += p
        else:
            merged.append(p)
    if len(merged) >= 2:
        return merged
    return _heuristic_split(w)


def token_syllables(token):
    """Display token -> list of (unit_index, char_start, char_end) syllables,
    char offsets inside that unit's string."""
    out = []
    for ui, u in enumerate(units(token)):
        k = 0
        for s in syllables(u):
            out.append((ui, k, k + len(s)))
            k += len(s)
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# output helpers

def r3(x):
    return round(float(x), 3)


def write_json(path, obj, compact=False):
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if compact:
        txt = json.dumps(obj, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False)
    else:
        txt = json.dumps(obj, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False, default=float)
    path.write_text(txt + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return path


def load_pron(path):
    if path:
        PRON_OVERRIDES.update(json.loads(Path(path).read_text()))

def expand(sections: list[Section], bpm: float, sr: int, syllables: dict) -> Score:
    g = Grid(bpm, sr)
    sec_rows, chords, bass, subs, pad, arps, melody, fx = [], [], [], [], [], [], [], []
    drum_hits: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]] = {}
    cutoff_pts: list[tuple[float, float]] = []
    gain_pts: list[tuple[float, float]] = []
    bar0 = 0
    for si, s in enumerate(sections):
        t0, t1 = g.t(bar0), g.t(bar0 + s.bars)
        tone_end = t1 - s.cut_last_beats * g.beat
        sec_rows.append(dict(name=s.name, label=s.label or s.name, start_bar=bar0, bars=s.bars,
                             start_s=t0, end_s=t1, start_sample=g.sample(t0), end_sample=g.sample(t1),
                             chords=list(s.chords)))

        for inst, pats in s.drums.items():

    B["delay"] = apply_gr(dl, gr, SIDECHAIN["delay"])
    timer("reverb+delay")
    B["gr_db"] = gr
    return B


def build_song_json(score: Score, n_samples: int, previews: dict) -> dict:
    g = Grid(score.bpm, score.sr)
    nbeats = score.total_bars * 4
    beats = [i * g.beat for i in range(nbeats)]
    melody = []
    for e in sorted(score.melody, key=lambda e: e["onset_s"]):
        melody.append(dict(onset_s=e["onset_s"], duration_s=e["duration_s"], onset_sample=g.sample(e["onset_s"]),
                           note=e["note"], midi=e["midi"], freq_hz=round(e["freq_hz"], 3), syllable=e["syllable"],
                           line=e["line"], lyric=e["lyric"], voice=e["voice"], section=e["section"],
                           primary=e["primary"]))
    hook = [m for m in melody if m["primary"]]
    lines = []
    for m in hook:
        if not lines or m["line"] != lines[-1]["line"] or m["onset_s"] - lines[-1]["_last"] > 0.5:
            lines.append(dict(line=m["line"], lyric=m["lyric"], section=m["section"], voice=m["voice"],
                              start_s=m["onset_s"], _last=m["onset_s"]))
        lines[-1]["_last"] = m["onset_s"]
        lines[-1]["end_s"] = m["onset_s"] + m["duration_s"]
    for l in lines:
        del l["_last"]
    return dict(
        title="It's 2am and I'm Crying", bpm=score.bpm, sample_rate=score.sr, beats_per_bar=4,
        beat_s=g.beat, bar_s=g.bar, key="A minor", total_bars=score.total_bars,
        music_end_s=score.music_end_s, duration_s=n_samples / score.sr, duration_samples=n_samples,
        time_origin="t=0 is the first sample of the WAV and the downbeat of bar 1; onset_sample = round(t*sr)",
        sections=score.sections,
        chords=score.chords,
        beats=beats, downbeats=beats[::4],
        kicks=[t for t, _ in score.drum_hits.get("kick", [])],
        claps=[t for t, _ in score.drum_hits.get("clap", [])],
        untz=[t for t, _ in score.drum_hits.get("untz", [])],
        hook_lines=lines,
        hook_notes=hook,
        melody_events=melody,
        previews=previews,
    )


def render_song(out_dir: Path, previews: bool = True, name: str = "2am_crying") -> dict:
    import song

    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    T0 = time.time()
    last = [T0]

    def timer(label):
        now = time.time()
        print(f"  {label:<14} {now - last[0]:6.2f} s")
        last[0] = now

    score = expand(song.SECTIONS, song.BPM, song.SR, song.SYLLABLES)
    timer("expand")
    B = render_buses(score, timer)
    gr = B.pop("gr_db")
    mix = sum(B.values())
    print(f"  pre-master peak {20*np.log10(np.max(np.abs(mix))):.1f} dBFS")
    y, minfo = master_chain(mix, song.TARGET_LUFS, song.CEILING_DBTP)
    timer("master")
    g = Grid(score.bpm, score.sr)
    # fade the very end of the reverb tail
    y[-int(0.5 * SR):] *= np.linspace(1, 0, int(0.5 * SR))[:, None]
    wav = out_dir / f"{name}.wav"
    sf.write(wav, y, SR, subtype="PCM_24")
    prev = {}
    if previews:
        secs = {s["name"]: s for s in score.sections}
        for key, sec in (("chorus", "chorus1"), ("bridge", "bridge")):
            st = secs[sec]["start_s"] - g.beat
            a, b = g.sample(st), g.sample(st + 10.0)
            clip = fade(y[a:b].copy(), 0.02, 0.4)
            p = out_dir / f"preview_{key}.wav"
            sf.write(p, clip, SR, subtype="PCM_24")
            prev[key] = dict(file=p.name, start_s=st, end_s=st + 10.0, section=sec)
    meta = build_song_json(score, y.shape[0], prev)
    meta["master"] = minfo
    meta["render_seconds"] = round(time.time() - T0, 2)
    (out_dir / "song.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, indent=1))
    timer("write")
    print(f"rendered {wav} ({y.shape[0]/SR:.2f} s) in {time.time() - T0:.1f} s")
    return meta

"""Demucs source separation, cached per input file in work/<audio-id>/stems/.

Demucs runs through its Python API on OUR gapless ffmpeg decode, so the stems
are already on the output timeline (the -23 ms LAME shift in pdoom-video was
needed only because the demucs CLI decoded the mp3 itself, without gapless
trimming).  The residual lag is measured anyway by cross-correlating the sum
of the stems against the input mix, logged, and corrected if non-zero.
"""
import common
import json

import numpy as np

SOURCES = ("drums", "bass", "other", "vocals")


def measure_lag(mix, est, sr, max_lag_s=0.06, win_s=20.0):
    """Lag (samples) of `est` relative to `mix` (positive = est is late), from
    FFT cross-correlation over the loudest windows."""
    n = len(mix)
    w = int(win_s * sr)
    if n <= w:
        starts = [0]
    else:
        e = np.array([np.sum(mix[s:s + w] ** 2) for s in range(0, n - w, w)])
        starts = [int(s) * w for s in np.argsort(e)[-3:]]
    m = int(max_lag_s * sr)
    lags = []
    for s in starts:
        a, b = mix[s:s + w], est[s:s + w]
        L = 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(len(a) + len(b))))
        c = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(b, L) * np.conj(np.fft.rfft(a, L)), L)
        c = np.concatenate([c[-m:], c[:m + 1]])
        lags.append(int(np.argmax(c)) - m)
    return int(np.median(lags))


def separate(audio_path, y=None, device=None, timer=None, force=False):
    """Returns dict name -> (2, n) float32 stems at 44.1 kHz on the gapless
    timeline, plus an info dict (lag, device, model)."""
    import soundfile as sf
    wd = common.work_dir(audio_path) / "stems"
    wd.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    info_p = wd / "info.json"
    if not force and info_p.exists() and all((wd / f"{s}.wav").exists() for s in SOURCES):
        info = json.loads(info_p.read_text())
        stems = {s: sf.read(wd / f"{s}.wav", dtype="float32", always_2d=True)[0].T.copy() for s in SOURCES}
        info["cached"] = True
        return stems, info
    if y is None:
        y = common.decode(audio_path)
    import torch
    from demucs.apply import apply_model
    from demucs.pretrained import get_model
    model = get_model(common.DEMUCS_MODEL)
    model.eval()
    assert model.samplerate == common.SR, model.samplerate
    wav = torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(y, dtype=np.float32))
    ref = wav.mean(0)
    mu, sd = ref.mean(), ref.std() + 1e-8
    x = ((wav - mu) / sd)[None]
    devices = [device] if device else (["mps", "cpu"] if torch.backends.mps.is_available() else ["cpu"])
    out, used, err = None, None, None
    for dev in devices:
        try:
            with torch.inference_mode():
                out = apply_model(model, x, device=dev, shifts=1, split=True, overlap=0.25, progress=False)[0]
            used = dev
            break
        except Exception as e:  # MPS op gaps -> fall back to CPU
            err = e
            print(f"demucs on {dev} failed ({type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:120]}), trying next device")
    if out is None:
        raise err
    out = (out * sd + mu).cpu().numpy().astype(np.float32)
    stems = {name: out[k] for k, name in enumerate(model.sources)}
    # timeline check: sum of stems vs the mix
    est = sum(stems.values()).mean(0)
    lag = measure_lag(y.mean(0), est, common.SR)
    if lag != 0:
        print(f"demucs stems lag {lag} samples -> shifting")
        for k in stems:
            stems[k] = np.roll(stems[k], -lag, axis=1)
            if lag > 0:
                stems[k][:, -lag:] = 0
            else:
                stems[k][:, :-lag] = 0
    for k, v in stems.items():
        sf.write(wd / f"{k}.wav", v.T, common.SR, subtype="FLOAT")
    info = dict(model=common.DEMUCS_MODEL, device=used, lag_samples=int(lag), lag_ms=round(lag / common.SR * 1000, 2))
    info_p.write_text(json.dumps(info))
    info["cached"] = False
    return stems, info

"""CTC emissions and one global constrained Viterbi forced alignment.

* Emissions: torchaudio MMS_FA (multilingual, romanised chars, made for
  alignment) and WAV2VEC2_ASR_LARGE_LV60K_960H (English chars), 20 ms frames,
  mapped to a shared alphabet (blank, a-z, ') and optionally fused as a
  probability mixture.
* All lines are aligned in ONE Viterbi pass over the whole song.  A garbage
  "star" token sits between lines (and at both ends): its per-frame score is
  (best token log-prob - margin), so it absorbs intros, ad-libs and backing
  vocals that are not in the sheet while lyric characters win wherever they
  really match.
"""
import common
import numpy as np
import numba

FRAME = 0.02  # s per emission frame (320 samples @ 16 kHz)
HOP = 320
ALPHA = ["-"] + list("abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz'")
AIDX = {c: i for i, c in enumerate(ALPHA)}
MODELS = {"mms": "MMS_FA", "lv60k": "WAV2VEC2_ASR_LARGE_LV60K_960H"}
_models = {}


def _bundle(name):
    import torchaudio
    return getattr(torchaudio.pipelines, MODELS[name])


def _model(name, device):
    key = (name, device)
    if key not in _models:
        b = _bundle(name)
        m = b.get_model(with_star=False) if name == "mms" else b.get_model()
        _models[key] = m.to(device).eval()
    return _models[key]


def raw_emissions(y16, name, device=None, chunk_s=20.0, ctx_s=3.0):
    """Frame-wise log-softmax of one model over a 16 kHz mono signal, computed
    on overlapping chunks (with context) and stitched.  [frames, vocab]."""
    import torch
    device = device or ("mps" if torch.backends.mps.is_available() else "cpu")
    model = _model(name, device)
    y = y16 / (np.abs(y16).max() + 1e-9)
    n_frames = len(y) // HOP
    chunk, ctx = int(chunk_s * 16000), int(ctx_s * 16000)
    out = None
    for s in range(0, len(y), chunk):
        a, b = max(0, s - ctx), min(len(y), s + chunk + ctx)
        x = torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(y[a:b], dtype=np.float32))[None].to(device)
        with torch.inference_mode():
            em, _ = model(x)
            em = torch.log_softmax(em, dim=-1)[0].float().cpu().numpy()
        if out is None:
            out = np.full((n_frames, em.shape[1]), np.nan, np.float32)
        f0 = a // HOP
        lo, hi = s // HOP, min(n_frames, (s + chunk) // HOP)
        seg = em[lo - f0: hi - f0]
        out[lo: lo + len(seg)] = seg
    good = np.where(~np.isnan(out[:, 0]))[0]
    out[good.max() + 1:] = out[good.max()]
    return out


def to_common(em, name):
    """Map a model's label set onto ALPHA (blank + word separator -> blank)."""
    labs = list(_bundle(name).get_labels(star=None) if name == "mms" else _bundle(name).get_labels())
    em = em.astype(np.float64)
    out = np.full((em.shape[0], len(ALPHA)), -1e4)
    blank_cols = [labs.index("-")] + ([labs.index("|")] if "|" in labs else [])
    out[:, 0] = np.logaddexp.reduce(em[:, blank_cols], axis=1)
    for i, c in enumerate(labs):
        k = c.lower()
        if k in AIDX and k != "-":
            out[:, AIDX[k]] = em[:, i]
    out -= np.logaddexp.reduce(out, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    return out


def emissions(work, sources, models=("mms", "lv60k"), timer=None):
    """sources: {tag: y16k}.  Returns {f"{model}_{tag}": logp [T, len(ALPHA)]},
    cached in work/emission_<model>_<tag>.npy."""
    out = {}
    for tag, y in sources.items():
        for m in models:
            p = work / f"emission_{m}_{tag}.npy"
            if p.exists():
                e = np.load(p)
            else:
                e = raw_emissions(y, m)
                np.save(p, e)
            out[f"{m}_{tag}"] = to_common(e, m)
    T = min(len(v) for v in out.values())
    return {k: v[:T] for k, v in out.items()}


def fuse(ems):
    es = list(ems)
    return np.logaddexp.reduce(np.stack(es), axis=0) - np.log(len(es))


@numba.njit(cache=True)
def _viterbi(E, tgt, lo, hi):
    T = E.shape[0]
    L = len(tgt)
    S = 2 * L + 1
    NEG = -1e18
    prev = np.full(S, NEG)
    cur = np.full(S, NEG)
    bp = np.zeros((T, S), np.int8)  # 0 stay, 1 from s-1, 2 from s-2
    prev[0] = E[0, 0]
    if lo[0] <= 0 <= hi[0]:
        prev[1] = E[0, tgt[0]]
    for t in range(1, T):
        for s in range(S):
            if s % 2 == 1:
                j = (s - 1) // 2
                if t < lo[j] or t > hi[j]:
                    cur[s] = NEG
                    continue
                e = E[t, tgt[j]]
            else:
                e = E[t, 0]
            best = prev[s]
            arg = 0
            if s >= 1 and prev[s - 1] > best:
                best = prev[s - 1]
                arg = 1
            if s >= 2 and s % 2 == 1:
                j = (s - 1) // 2
                if tgt[j] != tgt[j - 1] and prev[s - 2] > best:
                    best = prev[s - 2]
                    arg = 2
            cur[s] = best + e
            bp[t, s] = arg
        for s in range(S):
            prev[s] = cur[s]
    if prev[S - 1] >= prev[S - 2]:
        s = S - 1
    else:
        s = S - 2
    score = prev[s]
    path = np.zeros(T, np.int64)
    for t in range(T - 1, -1, -1):
        path[t] = s
        s -= bp[t, s]
    return path, score


def build_targets(lines_units):
    """lines_units: [[ [unit, ...] per token ] per line].
    Returns (tgt ids, index [(li, ti, ui, a, b)], star id)."""
    star = len(ALPHA)
    tgt, index = [star], []
    for li, toks in enumerate(lines_units):
        for ti, us in enumerate(toks):
            for ui, u in enumerate(us):
                a = len(tgt)
                tgt.extend(AIDX[c] for c in u)
                index.append((li, ti, ui, a, len(tgt)))
        tgt.append(star)
    return tgt, index, star


def align(E, lines_units, margin=1.5, line_windows=None):
    """Global alignment of all lines.  line_windows: {li: (t_lo, t_hi)} s.

    Returns {(li, ti): [dict(start, end, prob, chars=[(s, e), ...]) per unit]}, score."""
    T = E.shape[0]
    star_col = E.max(axis=1, keepdims=True) - margin
    Ex = np.concatenate([E, star_col], axis=1)
    tgt, index, star = build_targets(lines_units)
    tgt = np.array(tgt, np.int64)
    lo = np.zeros(len(tgt), np.int64)
    hi = np.full(len(tgt), T - 1, np.int64)
    if line_windows:
        for (li, ti, ui, a, b) in index:
            if li in line_windows:
                wl, wh = line_windows[li]
                lo[a:b] = np.maximum(lo[a:b], int(wl / FRAME))
                hi[a:b] = np.minimum(hi[a:b], int(wh / FRAME))
    if len(tgt) * 2 + 1 > T:
        raise SystemExit(f"lyrics too long for the audio ({len(tgt)} chars, {T} frames)")
    path, score = _viterbi(Ex, tgt, lo, hi)
    tokpos = np.where(path % 2 == 1, (path - 1) // 2, -1)
    P = np.exp(Ex[np.arange(T), np.where(tokpos >= 0, tgt[np.maximum(tokpos, 0)], 0)])
    spans = {}
    for (li, ti, ui, a, b) in index:
        chars = []
        for p in range(a, b):
            fr = np.where(tokpos == p)[0]
            chars.append((fr.min() * FRAME, (fr.max() + 1) * FRAME, float(P[fr].mean())))
        spans.setdefault((li, ti), []).append(dict(
            start=chars[0][0], end=chars[-1][1], prob=float(np.mean([c[2] for c in chars])),
            chars=[(c[0], c[1]) for c in chars]))
    return spans, float(score)

cat > /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/tools/make_test_vocal.py <<'EOF'
"""Synthesize a test "song" with exact line-level ground truth.

Each lyric line of the sheet is rendered with macOS `say` (one AIFF per line;
backing-vocal parentheticals in a second voice), silence-trimmed, and placed on
a 128 BPM beat grid with known gaps, so the ground-truth start/end of every
line is exact to the sample.  The vocal is mixed under a backing track:

  * the synth demo full-song WAV (songs/2am-crying/synth/out/*.wav + its
    song.json beat grid) if it exists, else
  * a 128 BPM kick + hat loop (+ a sustained chord pad, --no-pad to drop it)
    generated with ffmpeg aevalsrc.

Writes to --out (default shared/align/test/vocal/):
  mix.wav, mix.mp3 (libmp3lame 192k, LAME tag), vocal.wav, backing.wav,
  truth.json  {lines: [{i, text, section, start, end, runs}], bpm, beats, downbeats, ...}

  uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/tools/make_test_vocal.py \
      --lyrics songs/2am-crying/suno_lyrics_v1.txt
"""
import argparse
import json
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))
import common  # noqa: E402
import numpy as np  # noqa: E402
import soundfile as sf  # noqa: E402

SR = common.SR
SYNTH_OUT = common.REPO / "songs" / "2am-crying" / "synth" / "out"


def say(text, voice, rate, path):
    subprocess.run(["say", "-v", voice, "-r", str(rate), "-o", str(path), text], check=True)
    y = common.decode(path, sr=SR, mono=True)
    peak = np.abs(y).max() + 1e-9
    thr = peak * 10 ** (-45 / 20)
    idx = np.where(np.abs(y) > thr)[0]
    return y[idx[0]: idx[-1] + 1].astype(np.float32)


def find_synth():
    if not SYNTH_OUT.exists():
        return None, None
    wavs = [p for p in SYNTH_OUT.glob("*.wav") if not p.name.startswith("preview")]
    if not wavs:
        return None, None
    wav = max(wavs, key=lambda p: p.stat().st_size)
    sj = SYNTH_OUT / "song.json"
    return wav, (json.loads(sj.read_text()) if sj.exists() else None)


def aevalsrc_backing(dur, bpm, pad, path):
    b = 60.0 / bpm
    kick = f"sin(2*PI*(45*mod(t,{b})+3.5*(1-exp(-30*mod(t,{b})))))*exp(-9*mod(t,{b}))*0.9"
    hat = f"(random(0)*2-1)*exp(-70*mod(t+{b / 2},{b}))*0.22"
    expr = f"{kick}+{hat}"
    if pad:
        # A minor pad with a few harmonics, gently pulsing per beat (sidechain feel)
        chord = "+".join(f"{a}*sin(2*PI*{f}*t)" for f, a in
                         [(110, .5), (220, .6), (261.63, .5), (329.63, .5), (440, .35), (523.25, .25),
                          (659.26, .2), (880, .12), (1046.5, .08), (1318.5, .06)])
        expr += f"+0.09*({chord})*(1-0.6*exp(-6*mod(t,{b})))"
    subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-nostdin", "-v", "error", "-y", "-f", "lavfi", "-i",
                    f"aevalsrc='{expr}':s={SR}:d={dur:.3f}:c=mono", "-ac", "2", "-c:a", "pcm_f32le", str(path)],
                   check=True)
    return common.decode(path, sr=SR, mono=False)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--lyrics", default=str(common.REPO / "songs/2am-crying/suno_lyrics_v1.txt"))
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=str(common.ROOT / "test" / "vocal"))
    ap.add_argument("--voice", default="Samantha")
    ap.add_argument("--bv-voice", default="Daniel")
    ap.add_argument("--rate", type=int, default=185, help="say words per minute")
    ap.add_argument("--bpm", type=float, default=128.0)
    ap.add_argument("--vocal-db", type=float, default=-3.0,
                    help="vocal RMS (while singing) relative to backing RMS")
    ap.add_argument("--backing", choices=["auto", "synth", "loop"], default="auto")
    ap.add_argument("--no-pad", action="store_true", help="loop backing: kick + hat only")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    out = Path(args.out)
    tmp = out / "lines"
    tmp.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    lines = common.load_sheet(args.lyrics)
    synth_wav, synth_meta = find_synth() if args.backing in ("auto", "synth") else (None, None)
    if args.backing == "synth" and synth_wav is None:
        raise SystemExit(f"no synth WAV in {SYNTH_OUT}")
    if synth_wav is not None and synth_meta:
        bpm = float(synth_meta["bpm"])
        beats = [float(x) for x in synth_meta["beats"]]
    else:
        bpm = args.bpm
        beats = None
    beat = 60.0 / bpm

    # render runs
    rendered = []
    for ln in lines:
        runs, cur = [], None
        for w in ln["words"]:
            if cur is None or cur["bv"] != w["bv"]:
                cur = dict(bv=w["bv"], toks=[])
                runs.append(cur)
            cur["toks"].append(w["w"])
        segs = []
        for k, r in enumerate(runs):
            txt = re.sub(r"[()]", "", " ".join(r["toks"])).strip()
            y = say(txt, args.bv_voice if r["bv"] else args.voice, args.rate, tmp / f"line_{ln['i']:02d}_{k}.aiff")
            if r["bv"]:
                y = y * 10 ** (-4 / 20)
            segs.append((r["bv"], txt, y))
        rendered.append(segs)

    # place on the beat grid
    def next_beat(t):
        if beats is not None and t <= beats[-1]:
            return next(b for b in beats if b >= t - 1e-9)
        return np.ceil(t / beat - 1e-9) * beat
    cursor = 4 * beat * 2  # two bars of intro
    placed = []
    prev_sec = lines[0]["section"] if lines else ""
    for ln, segs in zip(lines, rendered):
        if ln["section"] != prev_sec:
            cursor += 4 * beat  # one extra bar at a section change
            prev_sec = ln["section"]
        t = next_beat(cursor)
        runs = []
        for bv, txt, y in segs:
            runs.append(dict(bv=bv, text=txt, start=t, end=t + len(y) / SR, y=y))
            t = t + len(y) / SR + 0.15
        placed.append(dict(i=ln["i"], text=ln["text"], section=ln["section"], start=runs[0]["start"],
                           end=runs[-1]["end"], runs=runs))
        cursor = runs[-1]["end"] + 0.35
    dur = placed[-1]["end"] + 4 * beat * 2

    vocal = np.zeros(int(dur * SR) + 1, np.float32)
    for p in placed:
        for r in p["runs"]:
            a = int(round(r["start"] * SR))
            vocal[a:a + len(r["y"])] += r["y"]
            r["start"] = a / SR
            r["end"] = (a + len(r["y"])) / SR
        p["start"], p["end"] = p["runs"][0]["start"], p["runs"][-1]["end"]

    # backing
    if synth_wav is not None:
        bk = common.decode(synth_wav, sr=SR, mono=False)
        src = f"synth:{synth_wav.relative_to(common.REPO)}"
        if bk.shape[1] < len(vocal):  # loop it if the TTS song is longer
            reps = int(np.ceil(len(vocal) / bk.shape[1]))
            bk = np.tile(bk, (1, reps))
        bk = bk[:, :len(vocal)]
    else:
        bk = aevalsrc_backing(dur, bpm, not args.no_pad, out / "backing_raw.wav")[:, :len(vocal)]
        src = "aevalsrc kick+hat" + ("" if args.no_pad else "+pad") + f" {bpm:g} BPM"
    if bk.shape[1] < len(vocal):
        bk = np.pad(bk, ((0, 0), (0, len(vocal) - bk.shape[1])))
    act = np.zeros(len(vocal), bool)
    for p in placed:
        for r in p["runs"]:
            act[int(r["start"] * SR):int(r["end"] * SR)] = True
    v_rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(vocal[act] ** 2))
    b_rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(bk.mean(0) ** 2)) + 1e-9
    g = b_rms / v_rms * 10 ** (args.vocal_db / 20)
    vocal *= g
    mix = bk + vocal[None]
    peak = np.abs(mix).max()
    mix = mix / peak * 10 ** (-1 / 20)
    vocal = vocal / peak * 10 ** (-1 / 20)
    sf.write(out / "mix.wav", mix.T, SR, subtype="PCM_16")
    sf.write(out / "vocal.wav", vocal, SR, subtype="PCM_16")
    sf.write(out / "backing.wav", (bk / peak * 10 ** (-1 / 20)).T, SR, subtype="PCM_16")
    subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-nostdin", "-v", "error", "-y", "-i", str(out / "mix.wav"), "-c:a", "libmp3lame",
                    "-b:a", "192k", str(out / "mix.mp3")], check=True)

    n_beats = int(dur / beat) + 1
    grid = beats if beats is not None else [k * beat for k in range(n_beats)]
    truth = dict(
        lyrics=str(Path(args.lyrics).resolve().relative_to(common.REPO)),
        voice=args.voice, bv_voice=args.bv_voice, rate=args.rate, vocal_db=args.vocal_db, backing=src,
        bpm=bpm, beats=[round(b, 6) for b in grid if b < dur],
        downbeats=[round(b, 6) for b in grid[::4] if b < dur], duration=round(len(vocal) / SR, 6),
        mp3_info=common.mp3_gapless_info(out / "mix.mp3"),
        lines=[dict(i=p["i"], text=p["text"], section=p["section"], start=round(p["start"], 6),
                    end=round(p["end"], 6),
                    runs=[dict(bv=r["bv"], text=r["text"], start=round(r["start"], 6), end=round(r["end"], 6))
                          for r in p["runs"]]) for p in placed])
    (out / "truth.json").write_text(json.dumps(truth, indent=1))
    print(f"{len(placed)} lines, {len(vocal) / SR:.1f} s, backing: {src}, mp3 {truth['mp3_info']}")
    print("wrote", out)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

cat > /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/align.py <<'EOF'
"""Word-level lyric alignment -> <out>/lyrics.json (+ align_debug.json, qa/*.png)

Pipeline (full mode)
  1. gapless ffmpeg decode of the song (common.decode) = the timeline.
  2. Demucs htdemucs_ft vocal stem (stems.py; lag vs. the mix measured, ~0).
  3. CTC emissions (20 ms frames) of the vocal stem from torchaudio MMS_FA and
     wav2vec2-large-lv60k-960h (mono sum, plus L/R channels when the stem is
     really stereo), fused as a probability mixture.
  4. ONE constrained Viterbi forced alignment over all lines with a garbage
     "star" token between lines (absorbs ad-libs / intros / unlisted vocals).
  5. signal refinement per sub-word unit on the vocal stem: voice re-entry
     after >= 50 ms silence, snap to a spectral-flux onset, pull fricatives
     back to the start of their noise; ends = next start when legato, else
     where the voice drops 15 dB.
  6. monotonic cleanup (starts increase by >= 20 ms, no overlaps).
  7. cross-check against mlx-whisper large-v3-turbo word timestamps
     (difflib SequenceMatcher on normalised units, matches > 1.5 s off rejected).
  8. conf per word = 0.35 + 0.30 model agreement (<= 60 ms) + 0.20 CTC posterior
     + 0.15 Whisper agreement (<= 150 ms).

Modes:  --no-demucs   same pipeline on the mix (no separation, no refinement)
        --whisper-only  Whisper word timestamps on the mix mapped onto the
                        sheet (unmatched words interpolated); fast preview.

  uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/align.py \
      --audio songs/<song>/audio/<file>.mp3 --lyrics songs/<song>/suno_lyrics_v1.txt \
      --out songs/<song>/data [--plots]
"""
import common  # noqa: I001  (must be first: cache env vars)
import argparse
import json
import re
import sys
from difflib import SequenceMatcher
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

import ctc

FEAT_SR = 24000
FEAT_HOP = 120  # 5 ms


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# signal features of the vocal source (5 ms hop)

def vocal_features(y):
    import librosa
    y = y / (np.abs(y).max() + 1e-9)
    rms = librosa.feature.rms(y=y, frame_length=512, hop_length=FEAT_HOP, center=True)[0]
    S = np.abs(librosa.stft(y, n_fft=1024, hop_length=FEAT_HOP, center=True)) ** 2
    fr = librosa.fft_frequencies(sr=FEAT_SR, n_fft=1024)
    sib = S[(fr > 4000) & (fr < 11000)].sum(0)
    low = S[(fr > 90) & (fr < 1500)].sum(0)
    mel = librosa.feature.melspectrogram(S=S, sr=FEAT_SR, n_mels=96, fmax=8000)
    onset = librosa.onset.onset_strength(S=librosa.power_to_db(mel, ref=np.max), sr=FEAT_SR,
                                         hop_length=FEAT_HOP, lag=2, max_size=3)
    n = min(len(rms), S.shape[1], len(onset))
    return dict(hop=FEAT_HOP / FEAT_SR, rms_db=librosa.amplitude_to_db(rms[:n], ref=1.0),
                sib=librosa.power_to_db(sib[:n] + 1e-10) - librosa.power_to_db(low[:n] + 1e-10),
                onset=onset[:n])


def runs(mask):
    m = np.concatenate([[False], mask, [False]]).astype(np.int8)
    d = np.diff(m)
    return list(zip(np.where(d == 1)[0], np.where(d == -1)[0]))


def activity(f, rel_db=22.0, abs_db=-48.0, win_s=1.5):
    from scipy.ndimage import maximum_filter1d, median_filter
    r = median_filter(f["rms_db"], 5)
    loc = maximum_filter1d(r, int(win_s / f["hop"]))
    return (r > abs_db) & (r > loc - rel_db), r


FRIC_START = re.compile(r"(s|sh|ch|z|f|th|j|c[eiy]|x|h)")
VOICED_TH = {"the", "there", "there's", "that", "that's", "they", "this", "then", "these", "those", "them",
             "than", "though", "thus", "they're", "they've"}
FRIC_END = re.compile(r"(s|z|f|x|ce|se|ze|sh|ch)$")


def refine(words, f):
    """Signal-based refinement of CTC unit starts and word ends (vocal stem)."""
    from scipy.ndimage import uniform_filter1d
    from scipy.signal import find_peaks
    hop = f["hop"]
    act, r = activity(f, rel_db=27.0)
    n = len(r)
    sil = ~act
    sib = uniform_filter1d(f["sib"], 3)
    on = f["onset"]
    pk_idx, _ = find_peaks(on, height=0.3 * np.percentile(on, 99), distance=int(0.04 / hop))
    units = []
    for k, w in enumerate(words):
        for j, u in enumerate(w["units"]):
            units.append(dict(k=k, j=j, text=u["text"], cs=u["start"], ce=u["end"], s=u["start"], rule="ctc"))
    for u_i, u in enumerate(units):
        pu = units[u_i - 1] if u_i else None
        prev_ce = pu["ce"] if pu else 0.0
        prev_cs = pu["cs"] if pu else 0.0
        s = u["cs"]
        # 1. rest-onset: voice re-enters after a >= 50 ms rest before the CTC start
        i0, i1 = int(prev_ce / hop), int(s / hop)
        done = False
        if i1 - i0 > int(0.05 / hop):
            rs = [(a, b) for a, b in runs(sil[i0:i1]) if (b - a) * hop >= 0.05]
            if rs:
                onset = (i0 + rs[-1][1]) * hop
                if s - onset > 0.04:
                    u["s"], u["rule"] = onset, "rest-onset"
                done = True
        # 2. onset snap (legato starts that CTC places late)
        if not done:
            vowel_init = u["text"][0] in "aeiou"
            lo = max(prev_ce - 0.06, prev_cs + 0.08, s - (0.25 if vowel_init else 0.12))
            hi = s + 0.04
            cand = [p for p in pk_idx if lo <= p * hop <= hi and np.median(sib[p:p + int(0.06 / hop)]) < -5]
            best, bsc = None, 0.0
            for p in cand:
                dt = s - p * hop
                wgt = 1.0 if dt < 0.06 else max(0.4, 1 - (dt - 0.06) / 0.4)
                if on[p] * wgt > bsc:
                    best, bsc = p, on[p] * wgt
            if best is not None:
                t = best * hop - 0.01
                if abs(t - s) > 0.02:
                    u["s"], u["rule"] = t, "onset-snap"
        # 3. fricative: CTC emits s/sh/f... at the END of the frication
        if FRIC_START.match(u["text"]) and u["text"] not in VOICED_TH:
            prev_fric_end = pu is not None and FRIC_END.search(pu["text"]) is not None
            lo_t = prev_ce + 0.02 if prev_fric_end else prev_ce - 0.10
            if pu is not None:
                lo_t = max(lo_t, pu["s"] + 0.10)
            lo = int(max(lo_t, s - 0.30, 0) / hop)
            a, b = max(int((s - 0.15) / hop), lo), int((s + 0.06) / hop)
            if b > a:
                pk = a + int(np.argmax(sib[a:b]))
                base = np.percentile(sib[max(0, lo - int(0.4 / hop)):lo + 1], 25) if lo > 0 else -40
                if sib[pk] >= base + 8:
                    thr = 0.5 * (base + sib[pk])
                    j = pk
                    while j - 1 >= lo and sib[j - 1] > thr:
                        j -= 1
                    if j * hop < u["s"] - 0.02:
                        u["s"], u["rule"] = j * hop, "fricative"
    for u_i in range(1, len(units)):
        units[u_i]["s"] = max(units[u_i]["s"], units[u_i - 1]["s"] + 0.03)
    for k, w in enumerate(words):
        us = [u for u in units if u["k"] == k]
        w["start"] = us[0]["s"]
        w["start_rule"] = us[0]["rule"]
        for j, u in enumerate(us):
            w["units"][j]["rstart"] = u["s"]
    # ends: next start when legato, else when the voice drops 15 dB below the word level
    for k, w in enumerate(words):
        nxt = words[k + 1]["start"] if k + 1 < len(words) else n * hop
        e = max(w["ctc_end"], w["start"] + 0.08)
        j0, j1 = int(w["start"] / hop), int(e / hop)
        level = np.percentile(r[j0:max(j1, j0 + 1)], 90)
        jn = int(nxt / hop)
        low = r < level - 15.0
        q, j, need = None, j1, int(0.06 / hop)
        while j < min(jn, n - need):
            if low[j] and low[j:j + need].all():
                q = j * hop
                break
            j += 1
        end = min(q, nxt) if q is not None else nxt
        end = max(end, w["start"] + 0.04)
        if nxt - end < 0.03:
            end = nxt
        w["end"] = end
    return words


def plain_ends(words, max_hold=1.5):
    """No refinement (mix mode): CTC start; end = CTC end, or the next start if
    the gap is tiny."""
    for k, w in enumerate(words):
        w["start_rule"] = "ctc"
        for u in w["units"]:
            u["rstart"] = u["start"]
        nxt = words[k + 1]["start"] if k + 1 < len(words) else w["ctc_end"] + max_hold
        end = min(max(w["ctc_end"], w["start"] + 0.06), nxt)
        if nxt - end < 0.06:
            end = nxt
        w["end"] = end
    return words


def monotonic(words, gap=0.02):
    for k in range(1, len(words)):
        if words[k]["start"] < words[k - 1]["start"] + gap:
            words[k]["start"] = words[k - 1]["start"] + gap
    for k in range(len(words)):
        if k + 1 < len(words) and words[k]["end"] > words[k + 1]["start"]:
            words[k]["end"] = words[k + 1]["start"]
        words[k]["end"] = max(words[k]["end"], words[k]["start"] + 0.01)
    return words


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Whisper

def run_whisper(y16, work, tag, prompt=None, force=False):
    import hashlib
    key = hashlib.sha1((prompt or "").encode()).hexdigest()[:8]
    p = work / f"whisper_{tag}_{key}.json"
    if p.exists() and not force:
        return json.loads(p.read_text())
    import mlx_whisper
    res = mlx_whisper.transcribe(
        np.ascontiguousarray(y16, dtype=np.float32), path_or_hf_repo=common.WHISPER_MODEL, language="en",
        word_timestamps=True, condition_on_previous_text=False, initial_prompt=prompt,
        temperature=(0.0, 0.2, 0.4), no_speech_threshold=None, hallucination_silence_threshold=2.0,
        verbose=None)
    ww = [dict(w=w["word"].strip(), start=float(w["start"]), end=float(w["end"]),
               p=float(w.get("probability", 0.0)))
          for s in res["segments"] for w in s.get("words", [])]
    out = dict(text=res.get("text", ""), words=ww)
    p.write_text(json.dumps(out, indent=1))
    return out


def match_whisper(tokens, ww):
    """Map whisper words onto lyric tokens through their normalised units.
    Returns list (per token) of whisper-word index ranges (first, last) or None."""
    a, a_tok = [], []
    for k, t in enumerate(tokens):
        for ui, u in enumerate(t["unit_texts"]):
            a.append(common.norm_word(u))
            a_tok.append((k, ui))
    b, b_w = [], []
    for j, w in enumerate(ww):
        for u in common.units(w["w"]):
            b.append(common.norm_word(u))
            b_w.append(j)
    sm = SequenceMatcher(a=a, b=b, autojunk=False)
    m = {}
    for tag, i1, i2, j1, j2 in sm.get_opcodes():
        if tag == "equal" or (tag == "replace" and i2 - i1 == j2 - j1):
            for d in range(i2 - i1):
                m[i1 + d] = b_w[j1 + d]
    out = [None] * len(tokens)
    for idx, (k, ui) in enumerate(a_tok):
        if idx in m:
            if out[k] is None:
                out[k] = [m[idx], m[idx], ui == 0]
            else:
                out[k][1] = m[idx]
    # a token counts as matched only if its first unit matched
    return [(o[0], o[1]) if o is not None and o[2] else None for o in out], set(m.values())


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def build_tokens(lines):
    toks = []
    for ln in lines:
        for ti, w in enumerate(ln["words"]):
            us = common.units(w["w"])
            toks.append(dict(li=ln["i"], ti=ti, w=w["w"], bv=w["bv"], unit_texts=us,
                             syl=common.token_syllables(w["w"])))
    return toks


def syllable_times(w):
    """[[start, end], ...] per syllable from the CTC char spans + refined unit starts."""
    if len(w["syl"]) <= 1:
        return None
    st = []
    for ui, c0, c1 in w["syl"]:
        u = w["units"][ui]
        st.append(u.get("rstart", u["start"]) if c0 == 0 else u["chars"][c0][0])
    st[0] = w["start"]
    for k in range(1, len(st)):
        st[k] = min(max(st[k], st[k - 1] + 0.02), w["end"] - 0.02 * (len(st) - k))
    return [[st[k], st[k + 1] if k + 1 < len(st) else w["end"]] for k in range(len(st))]


def even_syllables(w):
    if len(w["syl"]) <= 1:
        return None
    lens = np.array([c1 - c0 for _, c0, c1 in w["syl"]], float)
    edges = w["start"] + np.concatenate([[0], np.cumsum(lens)]) / lens.sum() * (w["end"] - w["start"])
    return [[edges[k], edges[k + 1]] for k in range(len(lens))]


def confidence(words, alts):
    for i, w in enumerate(words):
        ds = [abs(a[i]["start"] - w["ctc_start"]) for a in alts.values()]
        agree = float(np.mean([d <= 0.06 for d in ds])) if ds else 0.5
        p = min(1.0, w["ctc_prob"] / 0.5)
        wh = w.get("whisper")
        wagree = 0.5 if wh is None else float(abs(wh[0] - w["start"]) <= 0.15)
        w["conf_parts"] = dict(agree=round(agree, 2), ctc=round(p, 2), whisper=wagree)
        w["conf"] = round(float(np.clip(0.35 + 0.3 * agree + 0.2 * p + 0.15 * wagree, 0, 1)), 2)
    return words


def detect_extras(words, f, ww, used_w, have_stem):
    """Vocal activity / Whisper words not covered by any lyric word."""
    cand = []
    if f is not None and have_stem:
        hop = f["hop"]
        from scipy.ndimage import median_filter
        r = median_filter(f["rms_db"], 9)
        act = r > max(-40.0, np.percentile(r, 95) - 30)
        cover = np.zeros_like(act)
        for w in words:
            cover[max(0, int((w["start"] - 0.1) / hop)):int((w["end"] + 0.1) / hop)] = True
        for a, b in runs(act & ~cover):
            cand.append([a * hop, b * hop])
    for j, w in enumerate(ww):
        if j not in used_w and not any(x["start"] - 0.1 <= w["start"] <= x["end"] + 0.1 for x in words):
            cand.append([w["start"], w["end"]])
    cand.sort()
    merged = []
    for a, b in cand:
        if merged and a - merged[-1][1] < 0.4:
            merged[-1][1] = max(merged[-1][1], b)
        else:
            merged.append([a, b])
    out = []
    for a, b in merged:
        heard = [w["w"] for j, w in enumerate(ww) if j not in used_w and a - 0.05 <= w["start"] <= b + 0.05]
        if b - a < 0.5 and not heard:
            continue
        desc = "unlisted vocal (ad-lib / backing vocal / breath)" if have_stem else "unlisted vocal"
        if heard:
            desc += f'; Whisper hears: "{" ".join(heard)[:120]}"'
        out.append(dict(start=common.r3(a), end=common.r3(b), desc=desc))
    return out


def whisper_only(tokens, lines, ww):
    match, used = match_whisper(tokens, ww)
    for k, t in enumerate(tokens):
        mm = match[k]
        if mm is not None:
            a, b = ww[mm[0]], ww[mm[1]]
            t["start"], t["end"] = a["start"], b["end"]
            t["conf"] = round(0.4 + 0.5 * float(np.mean([w["p"] for w in ww[mm[0]:mm[1] + 1]])), 2)
            t["whisper"] = (a["start"], b["end"])
            t["src"] = "whisper"
    # enforce monotonic matches (drop matched tokens that go backwards in time)
    last = -1.0
    for t in tokens:
        if "start" in t:
            if t["start"] < last:
                for k in ("start", "end", "conf", "whisper", "src"):
                    t.pop(k, None)
            else:
                last = t["start"]
    # interpolate unmatched runs
    k = 0
    n = len(tokens)
    per_unit = 0.30
    while k < n:
        if "start" in tokens[k]:
            k += 1
            continue
        j = k
        while j < n and "start" not in tokens[j]:
            j += 1
        left = tokens[k - 1]["end"] if k > 0 else 0.0
        right = tokens[j]["start"] if j < n else left + per_unit * sum(len(t["syl"]) for t in tokens[k:j]) + 0.5
        run = tokens[k:j]
        # split the run at line boundaries: earlier lines hug the left anchor, later the right
        groups = []
        for t in run:
            if groups and groups[-1][0]["li"] == t["li"]:
                groups[-1].append(t)
            else:
                groups.append([t])
        need = [per_unit * sum(max(1, len(t["syl"])) for t in g) for g in groups]
        span = max(right - left, 0.05 * len(run))
        scale = min(1.0, span / (sum(need) + 1e-9))
        if len(groups) == 1 or sum(need) >= span:
            starts = [left + sum(need[:i]) * scale for i in range(len(groups))]
        else:
            # first group (continues the left line) hugs left unless it starts a new line
            same_left = k > 0 and tokens[k - 1]["li"] == groups[0][0]["li"]
            same_right = j < n and tokens[j]["li"] == groups[-1][-1]["li"]
            slack = span - sum(need)
            starts, pos = [], left
            for gi, g in enumerate(groups):
                if gi == 0 and same_left:
                    pass
                elif gi == len(groups) - 1 and same_right:
                    pos = right - need[gi]
                else:
                    pos += slack / (len(groups) + (0 if same_left else 1) - (1 if same_right else 0) + 1e-9)
                starts.append(pos)
                pos += need[gi]
        for g, s0, nd in zip(groups, starts, need):
            pos = s0
            for t in g:
                d = per_unit * max(1, len(t["syl"])) * scale
                t["start"], t["end"] = pos, pos + d * 0.9
                t["conf"] = 0.2
                t["src"] = "interpolated"
                pos += d
        k = j
    for t in tokens:
        t["ctc_start"] = t["start"]
    monotonic(tokens)
    for t in tokens:
        t["syl_t"] = even_syllables(t)
    return tokens, match, used


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_plots(words, lines, y_voc, sr, alts, ww, out_dir, label):
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    import librosa
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    colors = ["tab:red", "tab:blue", "tab:green", "tab:purple", "k"]
    for ln in lines:
        ws = [w for w in words if w["li"] == ln["i"]]
        t0 = max(0.0, min(ws[0]["start"], ws[0].get("ctc_start", ws[0]["start"])) - 0.8)
        t1 = min(max(ws[-1]["end"], ws[-1].get("ctc_end", ws[-1]["end"])) + 0.6, t0 + 10.0, len(y_voc) / sr)
        a, b = int(t0 * sr), int(t1 * sr)
        seg = y_voc[a:b]
        if len(seg) < 512:
            continue
        tracks = [("final", [(w["w"], w["start"], w["end"]) for w in words])]
        for name, aw in alts.items():
            tracks.append((name, [(w["w"], w["start"], w["end"]) for w in aw]))
        tracks.append(("whisper", [(w["w"], w["start"], w["end"]) for w in ww]))
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(3, 1, figsize=(20, 9), sharex=True,
                                 gridspec_kw=dict(height_ratios=[1.3, 2, 0.5 * len(tracks) + 0.5]))
        tt = t0 + np.arange(len(seg)) / sr
        axes[0].plot(tt, seg, color="0.3", lw=0.4)
        axes[0].set_ylabel(label)
        axes[0].set_title(f"L{ln['i']} [{ln['section']}]: {ln['text']}", fontsize=11)
        hop = 64
        S = librosa.amplitude_to_db(np.abs(librosa.stft(seg, n_fft=512, hop_length=hop)), ref=np.max)
        fr = librosa.fft_frequencies(sr=sr, n_fft=512)
        axes[1].imshow(S[fr <= 6000], origin="lower", aspect="auto", cmap="magma", vmin=-70, vmax=0,
                       extent=[t0, t0 + S.shape[1] * hop / sr, 0, 6000])
        axes[1].set_ylabel("Hz")
        ax3 = axes[2]
        for k, (name, items) in enumerate(tracks):
            yk = len(tracks) - k
            c = colors[k % len(colors)]
            for (lab, s, e) in items:
                if e < t0 or s > t1:
                    continue
                ax3.plot([s, e], [yk, yk], color=c, lw=5, alpha=0.6, solid_capstyle="butt")
                ax3.plot([s, s], [yk - 0.25, yk + 0.15], color=c, lw=1.5)
                ax3.text(s, yk + 0.18, lab, fontsize=9, color=c, clip_on=True)
                if k == 0:
                    for a_ in axes[:2]:
                        a_.axvline(s, color="c" if a_ is axes[1] else c, lw=0.9, alpha=0.9)
                elif name == "whisper":
                    axes[0].axvline(s, color=c, lw=0.8, ls=":", alpha=0.8)
            ax3.text(t0 + 0.01, yk - 0.35, name, fontsize=8, color=c)
        for w in ws:
            ax3.text(w["start"], 0.35, f"{w['conf']:.2f}", fontsize=7, color="0.4")
        ax3.set_ylim(0.2, len(tracks) + 0.8)
        ax3.set_yticks([])
        ax3.set_xlim(t0, t1)
        ax3.set_xticks(np.arange(np.ceil(t0 * 10) / 10, t1, 0.1), minor=True)
        ax3.grid(True, which="both", axis="x", alpha=0.3)
        fig.tight_layout()
        fig.savefig(out_dir / f"line_{ln['i']:02d}.png", dpi=70)
        plt.close(fig)


NOTES_FULL = (
    "Timeline = gapless ffmpeg decode of the input (LAME/Xing encoder delay trimmed, as browsers do). "
    "Method: Demucs {demucs} vocal stem (lag vs mix {lag} ms) -> CTC emissions (20 ms) from torchaudio "
    "MMS_FA + wav2vec2-large-lv60k-960h on {sources}, fused -> one global constrained Viterbi alignment of "
    "all lines with a garbage star token between lines -> signal refinement of word starts on the vocal "
    "stem (rest re-entry / spectral-flux onset / fricative noise start) and ends (legato or 15 dB drop) -> "
    "monotonic cleanup -> cross-check vs mlx-whisper large-v3-turbo word timestamps. "
    "conf = 0.35 + 0.30*model agreement(<=60 ms) + 0.20*CTC posterior + 0.15*Whisper agreement(<=150 ms). "
    "syl = per-syllable [start,end] (orthographic syllables timed by CTC char spans). "
    "bv = backing vocal (parenthesised on the sheet). {nlow} words have conf < 0.6; check qa plots.")
NOTES_MIX = (
    "Timeline = gapless ffmpeg decode of the input. Mode --no-demucs: CTC (MMS_FA + wav2vec2-lv60k, fused) "
    "aligned on the full mix, no signal refinement, Whisper cross-check on the mix. Less accurate than the "
    "full pipeline; {nlow} words have conf < 0.6.")
NOTES_WHISPER = (
    "Timeline = gapless ffmpeg decode of the input. Mode --whisper-only (coarse preview): mlx-whisper "
    "large-v3-turbo word timestamps on the mix, mapped onto the sheet with difflib SequenceMatcher; "
    "{nmatch}/{ntok} words matched, the rest interpolated (conf 0.2). syl split evenly by letters.")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--audio", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--lyrics", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--no-demucs", action="store_true", help="align on the mix (no separation)")
    ap.add_argument("--whisper-only", action="store_true", help="fast coarse mode: Whisper words mapped to the sheet")
    ap.add_argument("--plots", action="store_true", help="QA PNG per line in <out>/qa/")
    ap.add_argument("--pron", help='JSON {"Token": "spoken spelling"} overrides for odd words')
    ap.add_argument("--no-whisper", action="store_true", help="skip the Whisper cross-check")
    ap.add_argument("--whisper-prompt", default=None, help="initial prompt for Whisper")
    ap.add_argument("--margin", type=float, default=1.5, help="star-token margin (nats)")
    ap.add_argument("--force", action="store_true", help="recompute cached stems / emissions / whisper")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    common.load_pron(args.pron)
    T = common.Timer()
    out = Path(args.out)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    work = common.work_dir(args.audio)
    mode = "whisper-only" if args.whisper_only else "no-demucs" if args.no_demucs else "full"
    lines = common.load_sheet(args.lyrics)
    tokens = build_tokens(lines)
    dbg = dict(mode=mode, audio=str(Path(args.audio).resolve()), lyrics=str(Path(args.lyrics).resolve()),
               mp3=common.mp3_gapless_info(args.audio), work=str(work))

    with T("decode"):
        y = common.decode(args.audio)  # (2, n) @ 44.1k
        mix = y.mean(0)
        mix16 = common.resample(mix, common.SR, 16000)
    dbg["duration"] = round(len(mix) / common.SR, 3)

    # ---------------- whisper-only -----------------------------------------
    if mode == "whisper-only":
        with T("whisper"):
            W = run_whisper(mix16, work, "mix", args.whisper_prompt, args.force)
        ww = W["words"]
        with T("map"):
            words, match, used = whisper_only(tokens, lines, ww)
        nmatch = sum(m is not None for m in match)
        notes = NOTES_WHISPER.format(nmatch=nmatch, ntok=len(tokens))
        extras = detect_extras(words, None, ww, used, False)
        write_outputs(out, lines, words, extras, notes, dbg, T, ww, mode)
        if args.plots:
            with T("plots"):
                make_plots(words, lines, mix16, 16000, {}, ww, out / "qa", "mix")
        return

    # ---------------- CTC modes -------------------------------------------
    have_stem = mode == "full"
    if have_stem:
        import stems as stems_mod
        with T("demucs"):
            st, sinfo = stems_mod.separate(args.audio, y=y, force=args.force)
        dbg["stems"] = sinfo
        voc = st["vocals"]
        voc_m = voc.mean(0)
        srcs = {"voc": common.resample(voc_m, common.SR, 16000)}
        c = np.corrcoef(voc[0], voc[1])[0, 1] if np.std(voc[0]) > 0 and np.std(voc[1]) > 0 else 1.0
        dbg["vocal_lr_corr"] = round(float(c), 4)
        if c < 0.97:  # double-tracked / panned vocals: each channel is closer to a single voice
            srcs["vocL"] = common.resample(voc[0], common.SR, 16000)
            srcs["vocR"] = common.resample(voc[1], common.SR, 16000)
        feat_src = common.resample(voc_m, common.SR, FEAT_SR)
        plot_y = srcs["voc"]
    else:
        srcs = {"mix": mix16}
        feat_src = None
        plot_y = mix16
    dbg["ctc_sources"] = list(srcs)

    with T("ctc_emissions"):
        ems = ctc.emissions(work, srcs)
    E = ctc.fuse(ems.values())
    lines_units = [[t["unit_texts"] for t in tokens if t["li"] == ln["i"]] for ln in lines]

    def table(spans):
        res = []
        for t in tokens:
            sp = spans[(t["li"], t["ti"])]
            res.append(dict(start=sp[0]["start"], end=sp[-1]["end"], prob=float(np.mean([s["prob"] for s in sp])),
                            units=[dict(text=t["unit_texts"][k], start=s["start"], end=s["end"], chars=s["chars"])
                                   for k, s in enumerate(sp)]))
        return res

    with T("viterbi"):
        spans, score = ctc.align(E, lines_units, margin=args.margin)
        prim = table(spans)
        alts = {}
        # independent-ish alternatives for agreement: each model on the mono source
        tag0 = "voc" if have_stem else "mix"
        for m in ("mms", "lv60k"):
            s2, _ = ctc.align(ems[f"{m}_{tag0}"], lines_units, margin=args.margin)
            alts[m] = table(s2)
        if "vocL" in srcs:
            for side in ("vocL", "vocR"):
                s2, _ = ctc.align(ctc.fuse([ems[f"mms_{side}"], ems[f"lv60k_{side}"]]), lines_units,
                                  margin=args.margin)
                alts[side] = table(s2)
    dbg["viterbi_score"] = score
    words = []
    for t, p in zip(tokens, prim):
        w = dict(t)
        w.update(start=p["start"], end=p["end"], ctc_start=p["start"], ctc_end=p["end"], ctc_prob=p["prob"],
                 units=p["units"])
        words.append(w)

    if have_stem:
        with T("refine"):
            f = vocal_features(feat_src)
            words = refine(words, f)
    else:
        f = None
        words = plain_ends(words)
    words = monotonic(words)

    ww, used = [], set()
    if not args.no_whisper:
        with T("whisper"):
            W = run_whisper(srcs.get("voc", mix16), work, "voc" if have_stem else "mix", args.whisper_prompt,
                            args.force)
        ww = W["words"]
        match, used = match_whisper(words, ww)
        for w, mm in zip(words, match):
            x = None if mm is None else (ww[mm[0]]["start"], ww[mm[1]]["end"])
            if x is not None and abs(x[0] - w["start"]) > 1.5:
                x = None
            w["whisper"] = x
    words = confidence(words, alts)
    for w in words:
        w["syl_t"] = syllable_times(w)
    nlow = sum(w["conf"] < 0.6 for w in words)
    if have_stem:
        notes = NOTES_FULL.format(demucs=common.DEMUCS_MODEL, lag=dbg["stems"]["lag_ms"],
                                  sources="+".join(srcs), nlow=nlow)
    else:
        notes = NOTES_MIX.format(nlow=nlow)
    extras = detect_extras(words, f, ww, used, have_stem)
    dbg["alts"] = {k: [dict(start=common.r3(a["start"]), end=common.r3(a["end"])) for a in v] for k, v in alts.items()}
    write_outputs(out, lines, words, extras, notes, dbg, T, ww, mode)
    if args.plots:
        with T("plots"):
            make_plots(words, lines, plot_y, 16000, {k: [dict(w=t["w"], **a) for t, a in zip(tokens, v)]
                                                     for k, v in alts.items() if k in ("mms", "lv60k")},
                       ww, out / "qa", "vocal stem" if have_stem else "mix")
        write_json_timings(out, T)


def write_json_timings(out, T):
    p = out / "align_debug.json"
    d = json.loads(p.read_text())
    d["timings_s"] = T.t
    common.write_json(p, d)


def write_outputs(out, lines, words, extras, notes, dbg, T, ww, mode):
    doc_lines = []
    for ln in lines:
        ws = [w for w in words if w["li"] == ln["i"]]
        assert " ".join(w["w"] for w in ws) == ln["text"], (ln["text"], [w["w"] for w in ws])
        items = []
        for w in ws:
            d = dict(w=w["w"], start=common.r3(w["start"]), end=common.r3(w["end"]), conf=round(float(w["conf"]), 2))
            if w.get("syl_t"):
                d["syl"] = [[common.r3(a), common.r3(b)] for a, b in w["syl_t"]]
            if w["bv"]:
                d["bv"] = True
            items.append(d)
        doc_lines.append(dict(i=ln["i"], text=ln["text"], start=items[0]["start"], end=items[-1]["end"], words=items))
    doc = dict(lines=doc_lines, extras=extras, notes=notes)
    common.write_json(out / "lyrics.json", doc)
    keep = ("li", "ti", "w", "bv", "start", "end", "conf", "conf_parts", "ctc_start", "ctc_end", "ctc_prob",
            "start_rule", "whisper", "src", "unit_texts")
    dbg_words = []
    for w in words:
        d = {k: w[k] for k in keep if k in w}
        if "units" in w:
            d["units"] = [dict(text=u["text"], ctc=[common.r3(u["start"]), common.r3(u["end"])],
                               start=common.r3(u.get("rstart", u["start"]))) for u in w["units"]]
        dbg_words.append(d)
    dbg.update(lines=[dict(i=ln["i"], section=ln["section"], text=ln["text"]) for ln in lines],
               words=dbg_words, whisper_words=ww, timings_s=T.t)
    common.write_json(out / "align_debug.json", dbg)
    nlow = sum(w["conf"] < 0.6 for w in words)
    print(f"[{mode}] wrote {out / 'lyrics.json'}: {len(doc_lines)} lines, {len(words)} words "
          f"({nlow} conf<0.6), {len(extras)} extras; timings {T.t}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
