cat > $A/tools/eval_alignment.py <<'EOF'
"""Evaluate lyric alignment (and the beat grid) against the TTS test song.

  uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/tools/eval_alignment.py [--run] [--md]

--run   (re)runs align.py in every configuration on test/vocal/mix.{wav,mp3}
        (cached stems / emissions / whisper are reused unless --force)
--md    writes shared/align/EVAL.md

Metrics: absolute error of line starts (first word start vs. the first
audible sample of the TTS line), line ends, and the start of the backing-vocal
part inside a line (e.g. "(I'm crying)", rendered in a second voice), mean /
median / 95th percentile / max, in ms.
"""
import argparse
import json
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))
import common  # noqa: E402
import numpy as np  # noqa: E402

ROOT = common.ROOT
TEST = ROOT / "test" / "vocal"
OUT = ROOT / "test" / "out"
CONFIGS = [  # label, input, flags
    ("whisper-only (mix.wav)", "mix.wav", ["--whisper-only"], "whisper_wav"),
    ("no-demucs (mix.wav)", "mix.wav", ["--no-demucs"], "nodemucs_wav"),
    ("full + Demucs (mix.wav)", "mix.wav", [], "full_wav"),
    ("full + Demucs (mix.mp3)", "mix.mp3", [], "full_mp3"),
]


def run(cfg, force=False, plots=False):
    label, inp, flags, tag = cfg
    out = OUT / tag
    cmd = ["uv", "run", "--project", str(ROOT), "python", str(ROOT / "align.py"), "--audio", str(TEST / inp),
           "--lyrics", str(TEST / "lyrics.txt"), "--out", str(out)] + flags
    if force:
        cmd.append("--force")
    if plots:
        cmd.append("--plots")
    t0 = time.time()
    with open(OUT / f"{tag}.log", "w") as log:
        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    return time.time() - t0


def stats(err_s):
    e = np.abs(np.asarray(err_s)) * 1000
    if len(e) == 0:
        return dict(n=0)
    return dict(n=len(e), mean=float(e.mean()), median=float(np.median(e)), p95=float(np.percentile(e, 95)),
                max=float(e.max()), within100=float(np.mean(e <= 100) * 100))


def evaluate(truth, lyr):
    L = {ln["i"]: ln for ln in lyr["lines"]}
    s_err, e_err, bv_err, worst = [], [], [], []
    for g in truth["lines"]:
        p = L[g["i"]]
        s_err.append(p["start"] - g["start"])
        e_err.append(p["end"] - g["end"])
        worst.append((abs(p["start"] - g["start"]), g["i"], g["text"], p["start"] - g["start"]))
        # start of each later run (backing-vocal part) = first word of that run
        if len(g["runs"]) > 1:
            k = 0
            for ri, r in enumerate(g["runs"]):
                nw = len(r["text"].split())
                if ri > 0:
                    bv_err.append(p["words"][k]["start"] - r["start"])
                k += nw
    worst.sort(reverse=True)
    return dict(start=stats(s_err), end=stats(e_err), bv_start=stats(bv_err),
                signed_start_mean_ms=float(np.mean(s_err) * 1000), worst=worst[:5])


def eval_beats(truth):
    p = OUT / "analyze" / "audio.json"
    if not p.exists():
        return None
    a = json.loads(p.read_text())
    gb = np.array(truth["beats"])
    pb = np.array(a["beats"])
    gb = gb[(gb > 1.0) & (gb < truth["duration"] - 1.0)]
    d = np.array([np.min(np.abs(pb - b)) for b in gb]) * 1000 if len(pb) else np.array([np.inf])
    gd = np.array(truth["downbeats"])
    pd = np.array(a["downbeats"])
    gd = gd[(gd > 1.0) & (gd < truth["duration"] - 1.0)]
    dd = np.array([np.min(np.abs(pd - b)) for b in gd]) * 1000 if len(pd) else np.array([np.inf])
    return dict(bpm=a["bpm"], bpm_true=truth["bpm"], beat_err_mean_ms=float(d.mean()),
                beat_hit_rate_50ms=float(np.mean(d <= 50) * 100),
                downbeat_hit_rate_50ms=float(np.mean(dd <= 50) * 100), n_beats=len(pb),
                n_sections=len(a["sections"]), n_kicks=len(a["kicks"]))


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--run", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--force", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--plots", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--md", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--only", nargs="*", help="config tags to run")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    truth = json.loads((TEST / "truth.json").read_text())
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rows = []
    for cfg in CONFIGS:
        label, inp, flags, tag = cfg
        wall = None
        if args.run and (not args.only or tag in args.only):
            wall = run(cfg, args.force, args.plots)
        p = OUT / tag / "lyrics.json"
        if not p.exists():
            print("missing", p)
            continue
        dbg = json.loads((OUT / tag / "align_debug.json").read_text())
        r = evaluate(truth, json.loads(p.read_text()))
        r.update(label=label, tag=tag, wall=wall, timings=dbg.get("timings_s", {}))
        rows.append(r)
        s = r["start"]
        print(f"{label:28s} start mean {s['mean']:6.1f}  med {s['median']:6.1f}  p95 {s['p95']:6.1f}  "
              f"max {s['max']:7.1f} ms  <=100ms {s['within100']:5.1f}%  | end mean {r['end']['mean']:6.1f}  "
              f"| bv-part start mean {r['bv_start'].get('mean', float('nan')):6.1f} p95 "
              f"{r['bv_start'].get('p95', float('nan')):6.1f} (n={r['bv_start']['n']})  signed {r['signed_start_mean_ms']:+.1f}")
        for w in r["worst"][:3]:
            print(f"      worst L{w[1]:<3d} {w[3] * 1000:+8.1f} ms  {w[2][:60]}")
    beats = eval_beats(truth)
    if beats:
        print("analyze.py:", beats)
    (OUT / "eval_results.json").write_text(json.dumps(dict(rows=rows, beats=beats), indent=1, default=str))
    if args.md:
        write_md(rows, beats, truth)


def write_md(rows, beats, truth):
    def f(x):
        return f"{x:.0f}"
    t = ["| configuration | line-start mean | median | p95 | max | ≤100 ms | line-end mean | BV-part start mean / p95 | runtime |",
         "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    for r in rows:
        s, e, b = r["start"], r["end"], r["bv_start"]
        tot = sum(v for k, v in r["timings"].items() if k != "plots")
        t.append(f"| {r['label']} | **{f(s['mean'])} ms** | {f(s['median'])} | {f(s['p95'])} | {f(s['max'])} | "
                 f"{s['within100']:.0f}% | {f(e['mean'])} | {f(b['mean'])} / {f(b['p95'])} | {tot:.0f} s |")
    worst = []
    for r in rows:
        for w in r["worst"][:3]:
            worst.append(f"- {r['label']}: L{w[1]} `{w[2][:70]}` {w[3] * 1000:+.0f} ms")
    stage = []
    for r in rows:
        stage.append(f"- {r['label']}: " + ", ".join(f"{k} {v:.1f} s" for k, v in r["timings"].items()))
    bt = ""
    if beats:
        bt = (f"\n## Beat grid (analyze.py, no --sections, on mix.wav)\n\n"
              f"- tempo {beats['bpm']} BPM (truth {beats['bpm_true']})\n"
              f"- beats within 50 ms of a true beat: {beats['beat_hit_rate_50ms']:.0f}% "
              f"(mean distance {beats['beat_err_mean_ms']:.1f} ms, {beats['n_beats']} beats)\n"
              f"- true downbeats with a detected downbeat within 50 ms: {beats['downbeat_hit_rate_50ms']:.0f}%\n"
              f"- {beats['n_sections']} sections, {beats['n_kicks']} kicks\n")
    md = f"""# Alignment evaluation

Generated by `tools/eval_alignment.py --run --md` on the synthetic test song from
`tools/make_test_vocal.py`: {len(truth['lines'])} lines of `{truth['lyrics']}` rendered line by line with
macOS `say` (lead voice {truth['voice']}, parenthesised backing vocals in {truth['bv_voice']}, {truth['rate']} wpm),
placed on the {truth['bpm']:g} BPM beat grid with known gaps, and mixed {truth['vocal_db']:+g} dB (vocal RMS vs.
backing RMS) under the backing `{truth['backing']}`. Duration {truth['duration']:.0f} s.
Ground truth per line = first / last audible sample of the TTS render (exact). "BV-part start" = start of the
second-voice part inside a line, i.e. the first word of e.g. `(I'm crying)`.

{chr(10).join(t)}

Runtime = sum of the stage timings with warm model caches (first runs also download models, see README).
Stems / emissions / Whisper output are cached per input file, so the mp3 row re-computes everything (different
file) while the no-demucs and whisper-only rows reuse nothing from the full run.

Per-stage timings (s):
{chr(10).join(stage)}

Worst lines:
{chr(10).join(worst)}
{bt}
## Caveats (read before trusting these numbers)

- Spoken TTS is much easier than a sung Suno vocal: clean consonant onsets, no melisma, no held vowels, no
  reverb tails, no doubled / harmonised lead, no ad-libs.  Expect real songs to be noticeably worse, mainly on
  long legato notes, buried choruses and ad-lib-heavy sections; check the `--plots` QA images.
- The ground truth is line level only (plus the start of each backing-vocal part).  Word-level accuracy is not
  measured here; on clean speech the CTC + refinement word starts typically agree with Whisper to ~50 ms
  (see `conf` / `align_debug.json`).
- The backing here is a synthetic track; vocal-vs-backing masking in a real mix is different.
"""
    (ROOT / "EVAL.md").write_text(md)
    print("wrote", ROOT / "EVAL.md")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

BashWrite analyze.py and check align run11:41 PM
input
description Write analyze.py and check align run
cat > /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/analyze.py <<'EOF'
"""Music analysis -> <out>/audio.json (+ audio_debug.json)

  * beats: librosa beat_track on drum-stem + mix onset strength, then (when
    the tempo is steady) a constant-tempo grid fitted to the onsets and phase
    refined on kick attacks; downbeats: bar phase with the strongest
    bar-level evidence (low-band onsets + chord changes, snare/clap backbeat
    counted against);
  * sections: Laplacian structural segmentation (librosa recurrence +
    spectral clustering), boundaries snapped to downbeats, named s1, s2, ...;
  * kicks: attacks of the <150 Hz band of the Demucs drum stem (or the mix);
  * vocal_onsets: log-mel flux peaks of the vocal stem while the voice is active;
  * loudness: mix RMS in dBFS every 10 ms.

With --sections song.json (bpm, sections with start/end, beats[, downbeats])
the beat grid and sections are taken from the file as the truth and only
kicks / vocal onsets / loudness are computed.

  uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/analyze.py \
      --audio songs/<song>/audio/<file>.mp3 --out songs/<song>/data [--sections song.json]
"""
import common  # noqa: I001
import argparse
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from scipy.ndimage import maximum_filter1d, median_filter, uniform_filter1d
from scipy.signal import butter, find_peaks, sosfiltfilt

SR = common.SR
LOUD_HOP = 0.01


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def band_sos(lo, hi, sr):
    if lo and hi:
        return butter(4, [lo, hi], btype="band", fs=sr, output="sos")
    if hi:
        return butter(4, hi, btype="low", fs=sr, output="sos")
    return butter(4, lo, btype="high", fs=sr, output="sos")


def band_onsets(x, sr, lo, hi, win=0.010, hop_s=0.002, min_gap=0.08, rel_db=10.0):
    """Attacks in a band: steepest rise of the band's log-energy envelope.
    Returns (times, peak_db)."""
    xb = sosfiltfilt(band_sos(lo, hi, sr), x)
    h = int(hop_s * sr)
    w = int(win * sr)
    e = np.convolve(xb.astype(np.float64) ** 2, np.ones(w) / w, mode="same")[::h]
    db = 10 * np.log10(e + 1e-10)
    fps = sr / h
    d = uniform_filter1d(np.diff(db, prepend=db[0]), 3)
    lag = int(0.02 * fps)
    rise = db - np.concatenate([np.full(lag, db[0]), db[:-lag]])
    floor = median_filter(db, int(1.0 * fps) | 1)
    gmax = np.percentile(db, 99.5)
    pk, _ = find_peaks(rise, height=rel_db, distance=int(min_gap * fps))
    times, strength = [], []
    for p in pk:
        a = max(0, p - lag)
        q = a + int(np.argmax(d[a:p + 1]))
        peak_db = db[p:p + int(0.03 * fps)].max()
        if peak_db < floor[p] + 3 or peak_db < gmax - 35:
            continue
        times.append(q / fps)
        strength.append(peak_db)
    return np.array(times), np.array(strength)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def onset_env(x, sr=22050, hop=256):
    import librosa
    return librosa.onset.onset_strength(y=x, sr=sr, hop_length=hop, lag=1, max_size=1)


def beat_grid(mix22, drums22, kicks, duration, sr=22050, hop=256):
    """Returns (bpm, beats[], info)."""
    import librosa
    o = onset_env(mix22, sr, hop)
    o = o / (np.percentile(o, 99) + 1e-9)
    if drums22 is not None:
        od = onset_env(drums22, sr, hop)
        n = min(len(o), len(od))
        o = o[:n] + od[:n] / (np.percentile(od, 99) + 1e-9)
    fps = sr / hop
    tempo, bt = librosa.beat.beat_track(onset_envelope=o, sr=sr, hop_length=hop, units="time", tightness=200)
    tempo = float(np.atleast_1d(tempo)[0])
    info = dict(librosa_tempo=round(tempo, 3), librosa_beats=len(bt))
    if len(bt) < 8:
        return tempo, bt, info
    ibi = np.diff(bt)
    P0 = float(np.median(ibi))
    cv = float(np.std(ibi[(ibi > 0.7 * P0) & (ibi < 1.3 * P0)]) / P0)
    info["ibi_cv"] = round(cv, 4)

    def score(P, off):
        ts = off + P * np.arange(int((duration - off) / P) + 1)
        idx = np.round(ts * fps).astype(int)
        idx = idx[(idx > 2) & (idx < len(o) - 2)]
        return np.maximum.reduce([o[idx - 1], o[idx], o[idx + 1]]).mean()

    if cv > 0.06:  # not steady: keep librosa's (possibly drifting) beats
        info["grid"] = "librosa"
        return 60 / P0, bt, info
    # constant grid: coarse search around the librosa period, then fine
    best = (-1, P0, 0.0)
    for P in P0 * np.linspace(0.985, 1.015, 61):
        for off in np.arange(0, P, 0.005):
            s = score(P, off)
            if s > best[0]:
                best = (s, P, off)
    _, P, off = best
    for P2 in np.linspace(P * 0.9995, P * 1.0005, 21):
        for o2 in np.arange(off - 0.006, off + 0.006, 0.001):
            s = score(P2, o2)
            if s > best[0]:
                best = (s, P2, o2)
    _, P, off = best
    # phase refinement on kick attacks
    if len(kicks):
        n_ = np.round((kicks - off) / P)
        res = kicks - (off + n_ * P)
        res = res[np.abs(res) < 0.06]
        if len(res) >= 8:
            off += float(np.median(res))
            info["kick_residual_ms"] = round(float(np.std(res)) * 1000, 2)
    # prefer a "musical" tempo range (70-180)
    while 60 / P < 70:
        P /= 2
    while 60 / P > 180:
        P *= 2
    off = off - P * math.floor(off / P)
    beats = off + P * np.arange(int((duration - off) / P) + 1)
    info.update(grid="constant", period=round(P, 6), first_beat=round(off, 4))
    return 60 / P, beats, info


def downbeat_phase(beats, mix22, sr=22050, hop=256, bpb=4):
    """Bar phase from bar-level evidence: low-band onset strength (kick/bass
    note on the one) + chord change (beat-synchronous chroma novelty), minus
    the snare/clap backbeat (2 and 4)."""
    import librosa
    fps = sr / hop
    low = librosa.onset.onset_strength(y=sosfiltfilt(band_sos(None, 150, sr), mix22), sr=sr, hop_length=hop)
    hi = librosa.onset.onset_strength(y=sosfiltfilt(band_sos(1500, 6000, sr), mix22), sr=sr, hop_length=hop)
    ch = librosa.feature.chroma_cqt(y=mix22, sr=sr, hop_length=hop)
    bf = np.clip(np.round(np.asarray(beats) * fps).astype(int), 0, ch.shape[1] - 1)
    sync = librosa.util.sync(ch, bf, aggregate=np.median)  # chroma per beat interval
    sync = sync / (np.linalg.norm(sync, axis=0, keepdims=True) + 1e-9)
    nov = np.zeros(len(bf))
    # chord change at beat k: compare the 2 beats before vs the 2 beats after
    for k in range(2, len(bf) - 2):
        a = sync[:, k - 1:k + 1].mean(1) if k < sync.shape[1] else sync[:, -1]
        b = sync[:, k + 1:k + 3].mean(1) if k + 3 <= sync.shape[1] else sync[:, -1]
        nov[k] = 1 - float(a @ b / (np.linalg.norm(a) * np.linalg.norm(b) + 1e-9))

    def at(env, k):
        i = bf[k]
        return env[max(0, i - 2):i + 3].max() if i < len(env) else 0.0

    lo_b = np.array([at(low, k) for k in range(len(bf))])
    hi_b = np.array([at(hi, k) for k in range(len(bf))])

    def z(x):
        return (x - x.mean()) / (x.std() + 1e-9)
    ev = z(lo_b) + 1.5 * z(nov) - 0.5 * z(hi_b)
    scores = [float(ev[p::bpb].mean()) for p in range(bpb)]
    return int(np.argmax(scores)), scores


def segment(mix22, beats, downbeats, duration, k=None, sr=22050, hop=512, min_bars=4):
    """Laplacian structural segmentation (McFee & Ellis 2014, librosa gallery),
    boundaries snapped to downbeats."""
    import librosa
    import scipy
    from sklearn.cluster import KMeans
    C = librosa.amplitude_to_db(np.abs(librosa.cqt(y=mix22, sr=sr, hop_length=hop, bins_per_octave=36,
                                                   n_bins=7 * 36)), ref=np.max)
    bf = librosa.time_to_frames(np.asarray(beats), sr=sr, hop_length=hop)
    bf = librosa.util.fix_frames(bf, x_min=0, x_max=C.shape[1])
    Cs = librosa.util.sync(C, bf, aggregate=np.median)
    mf = librosa.feature.mfcc(y=mix22, sr=sr, hop_length=hop, n_mfcc=13)
    Ms = librosa.util.sync(mf, bf)
    R = librosa.segment.recurrence_matrix(Cs, width=3, mode="affinity", sym=True)
    Rf = librosa.segment.timelag_filter(scipy.ndimage.median_filter)(R, size=(1, 7))
    path_d = np.sum(np.diff(Ms, axis=1) ** 2, axis=0)
    sigma = np.median(path_d) + 1e-9
    path_sim = np.exp(-path_d / sigma)
    R_path = np.diag(path_sim, k=1) + np.diag(path_sim, k=-1)
    deg_path = np.sum(R_path, axis=1)
    deg_rec = np.sum(Rf, axis=1)
    mu = deg_path.dot(deg_path + deg_rec) / (np.sum((deg_path + deg_rec) ** 2) + 1e-9)
    A = mu * Rf + (1 - mu) * R_path
    L = scipy.sparse.csgraph.laplacian(A, normed=True)
    evals, evecs = scipy.linalg.eigh(L)
    evecs = scipy.ndimage.median_filter(evecs, size=(9, 1))
    Cn = np.cumsum(evecs ** 2, axis=1) ** 0.5
    if k is None:  # eigengap heuristic in 3..8
        gaps = np.diff(evals[:10])
        k = int(np.clip(np.argmax(gaps[2:8]) + 3, 3, 8))
    X = evecs[:, :k] / (Cn[:, k - 1:k] + 1e-9)
    labels = KMeans(n_clusters=k, n_init=10, random_state=0).fit_predict(X)
    bnd_beats = 1 + np.flatnonzero(labels[:-1] != labels[1:])
    # beat-sync frame k covers bf[k]..bf[k+1]; frame 0 starts at t=0 (fix_frames added 0)
    bt = librosa.frames_to_time(bf, sr=sr, hop_length=hop)
    cand = [bt[b] for b in bnd_beats if b < len(bt)]
    db = np.asarray(downbeats)
    snapped = sorted(set(float(db[np.argmin(np.abs(db - t))]) for t in cand)) if len(db) else cand
    bar = (np.median(np.diff(db)) if len(db) > 1 else 2.0)
    bounds = [0.0]
    for t in snapped:
        if t - bounds[-1] >= min_bars * bar - 1e-3 and duration - t >= min_bars * bar * 0.5:
            bounds.append(t)
    bounds.append(duration)
    secs = [dict(name=f"s{i + 1}", start=common.r3(a), end=common.r3(b)) for i, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(bounds, bounds[1:]))]
    return secs, dict(k=k, labels=labels.tolist(), raw_boundaries=[common.r3(t) for t in cand])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def vocal_onsets(v, sr=SR):
    import librosa
    y = common.resample(v, sr, 22050)
    y = y / (np.abs(y).max() + 1e-9)
    hop = 110
    hs = hop / 22050
    rms = librosa.amplitude_to_db(librosa.feature.rms(y=y, frame_length=1024, hop_length=hop)[0], ref=1.0)
    S = np.abs(librosa.stft(y, n_fft=1024, hop_length=hop)) ** 2
    mel = librosa.feature.melspectrogram(S=S, sr=22050, n_mels=96, fmax=8000)
    on = librosa.onset.onset_strength(S=librosa.power_to_db(mel, ref=np.max), sr=22050, hop_length=hop, lag=2,
                                      max_size=3)
    n = min(len(on), len(rms))
    on, rms = on[:n], rms[:n]
    loc = maximum_filter1d(rms, int(2.0 / hs))
    active = (rms > -45) & (rms > loc - 25)
    thr = uniform_filter1d(on, int(0.4 / hs)) * 1.5 + 0.15 * np.percentile(on, 99)
    pk, _ = find_peaks(on, height=0, distance=int(0.09 / hs))
    return [float(p * hs) for p in pk if on[p] > thr[p] and active[min(n - 1, p + int(0.03 / hs))]]


def loudness(x, sr=SR, hop_s=LOUD_HOP, win=2048):
    hop = sr * hop_s
    n = int(math.ceil(len(x) / sr / hop_s))
    pad = np.pad(x.astype(np.float64), (win // 2, win // 2 + int(hop) + 2))
    idx = (np.arange(n) * hop).astype(int)
    c = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(pad ** 2)])
    e = (c[idx + win] - c[idx]) / win
    return [round(float(v), 1) for v in np.maximum(10 * np.log10(np.maximum(e, 1e-12)), -90.0)]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _first(d, keys, default=None):
    for k in keys:
        if isinstance(d, dict) and d.get(k) is not None:
            return d[k]
    return default


def load_structure(path, duration):
    """Tolerant reader for a known structure file (e.g. the synth's song.json):
    bpm|tempo; sections [{name|label, start|start_s|t0, end|end_s|t1}];
    beats|beat_grid (floats or {t|time|start}); downbeats or every
    beats_per_bar-th beat."""
    d = json.loads(Path(path).read_text())
    if isinstance(d, list):
        d = dict(sections=d)
    bpm = _first(d, ("bpm", "tempo", "BPM"))
    bpb = int(_first(d, ("beats_per_bar", "time_signature", "meter"), 4) or 4)

    def times(v):
        if v is None:
            return None
        out = []
        for x in v:
            out.append(float(_first(x, ("t", "time", "start", "start_s", "onset_s"))) if isinstance(x, dict) else float(x))
        return out
    beats = times(_first(d, ("beats", "beat_grid", "beat_times", "grid")))
    downbeats = times(_first(d, ("downbeats", "downbeat_times", "bars")))
    if beats is None and bpm:
        P = 60.0 / float(bpm)
        off = float(_first(d, ("first_beat", "offset", "beat_offset", "start_offset"), 0.0))
        beats = list(off + P * np.arange(int((duration - off) / P) + 1))
    if beats is not None and downbeats is None:
        downbeats = beats[::bpb]
    if bpm is None and beats is not None and len(beats) > 1:
        bpm = 60.0 / float(np.median(np.diff(beats)))
    secs = []
    raw = _first(d, ("sections", "structure", "parts"), []) or []
    for s in raw:
        st = _first(s, ("start", "start_s", "t0", "begin", "time"))
        if st is None and "start_bar" in s and downbeats:
            st = downbeats[min(int(s["start_bar"]), len(downbeats) - 1)]
        en = _first(s, ("end", "end_s", "t1", "stop"))
        secs.append(dict(name=str(_first(s, ("name", "label", "section", "id"), f"s{len(secs) + 1}")),
                         start=float(st), end=None if en is None else float(en)))
    secs.sort(key=lambda s: s["start"])
    for i, s in enumerate(secs):
        if s["end"] is None:
            s["end"] = secs[i + 1]["start"] if i + 1 < len(secs) else duration
    return dict(bpm=None if bpm is None else float(bpm), beats=beats, downbeats=downbeats, sections=secs)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--audio", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--sections", help="structure JSON (e.g. synth song.json): beat grid + sections as truth")
    ap.add_argument("--no-demucs", action="store_true", help="no stems: kicks from the mix, no vocal onsets")
    ap.add_argument("--k", type=int, default=None, help="number of section clusters (default: eigengap)")
    ap.add_argument("--plots", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--force", action="store_true")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    import librosa
    T = common.Timer()
    out = Path(args.out)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    dbg = dict(audio=str(Path(args.audio).resolve()), mp3=common.mp3_gapless_info(args.audio))
    with T("decode"):
        y = common.decode(args.audio)
        mix = y.mean(0)
        duration = len(mix) / SR
        mix22 = common.resample(mix, SR, 22050)
    st = None
    if not args.no_demucs:
        import stems as stems_mod
        with T("demucs"):
            st, sinfo = stems_mod.separate(args.audio, y=y, force=args.force)
        dbg["stems"] = sinfo
    drums = st["drums"].mean(0) if st else mix
    with T("kicks"):
        kt, kdb = band_onsets(drums, SR, None, 150, win=0.012, min_gap=0.1, rel_db=12)
    known = load_structure(args.sections, duration) if args.sections else None
    with T("beats"):
        if known and known["beats"]:
            beats = np.array(known["beats"])
            downbeats = np.array(known["downbeats"])
            bpm = known["bpm"]
            dbg["grid"] = dict(source=str(Path(args.sections).resolve()))
        else:
            d22 = common.resample(drums, SR, 22050) if st else None
            bpm, beats, ginfo = beat_grid(mix22, d22, kt, duration)
            phase, scores = downbeat_phase(beats, mix22)
            downbeats = np.asarray(beats)[phase::4]
            ginfo.update(downbeat_phase=phase, phase_scores=[round(s, 3) for s in scores])
            dbg["grid"] = ginfo
    with T("sections"):
        if known and known["sections"]:
            sections = [dict(name=s["name"], start=common.r3(s["start"]), end=common.r3(s["end"]))
                        for s in known["sections"]]
        else:
            sections, sinf = segment(mix22, beats, downbeats, duration, k=args.k)
            dbg["segmentation"] = sinf
    with T("vocal_onsets"):
        vo = vocal_onsets(st["vocals"].mean(0)) if st else []
    with T("loudness"):
        rms_db = loudness(mix)
    doc = dict(bpm=round(float(bpm), 3), beats=[common.r3(b) for b in beats if b <= duration],
               downbeats=[common.r3(b) for b in downbeats if b <= duration], sections=sections,
               kicks=[common.r3(t) for t in kt], vocal_onsets=[common.r3(t) for t in vo],
               loudness=dict(hop=LOUD_HOP, rms_db=rms_db), duration=common.r3(duration))
    common.write_json(out / "audio.json", doc, compact=True)
    dbg["timings_s"] = T.t
    common.write_json(out / "audio_debug.json", dbg)
    print(f"wrote {out / 'audio.json'}: {doc['bpm']} BPM, {len(doc['beats'])} beats, {len(doc['downbeats'])} "
          f"downbeats, {len(sections)} sections, {len(doc['kicks'])} kicks, {len(vo)} vocal onsets; {T.t}")
    if args.plots:
        plot_overview(doc, out / "qa" / "audio_overview.png")


def plot_overview(doc, path):
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    t = np.arange(len(doc["loudness"]["rms_db"])) * doc["loudness"]["hop"]
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(1, 1, figsize=(24, 5))
    ax.plot(t, doc["loudness"]["rms_db"], color="k", lw=0.5)
    for b in doc["downbeats"]:
        ax.axvline(b, color="0.7", lw=0.5)
    for k in doc["kicks"]:
        ax.plot([k], [-3], "v", color="tab:red", ms=2)
    for v in doc["vocal_onsets"]:
        ax.plot([v], [-6], "v", color="tab:purple", ms=2)
    for s in doc["sections"]:
        ax.axvline(s["start"], color="tab:blue", lw=2)
        ax.text(s["start"] + 0.3, -1, s["name"], color="tab:blue")
    ax.set_ylim(-60, 0)
    ax.set_xlim(0, doc["duration"])
    ax.set_title(f"{doc['bpm']} BPM")
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(path, dpi=70)
    plt.close(fig)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

tgt, index, star = ctc.build_targets(lu)
tgt = np.array(tgt)
path, score = ctc._viterbi(Ex, tgt, np.zeros(len(tgt), np.int64), np.full(len(tgt), T-1, np.int64))
tokpos = np.where(path % 2 == 1, (path - 1) // 2, -1)
for li in [int(x) for x in sys.argv[1:]]:
    for (l, ti, ui, a, b) in index:
        if l != li: continue
        s = []
        for p in range(a, b):

        print(li, ti, s)

p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/align.py'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace('''def monotonic(words, gap=0.02):''','''def apply_fixes(words, path):
    """Manual corrections after looking at the QA plots.  JSON:
    {"<line>:<word>": {"start": s, "end": e, "conf": c}, "<line>": {"shift": dt}}
    (0-based indices as in lyrics.json)."""
    if not path:
        return words, 0
    fx = json.loads(Path(path).read_text())
    n = 0
    for key, v in fx.items():
        if key.startswith("_"):
            continue
        if ":" in key:
            li, ti = (int(x) for x in key.split(":"))
            sel = [w for w in words if w["li"] == li and w["ti"] == ti]
        else:
            sel = [w for w in words if w["li"] == int(key)]
        for w in sel:
            if "shift" in v:
                w["start"] += v["shift"]
                w["end"] += v["shift"]
            if "start" in v:
                w["start"] = float(v["start"])
            if "end" in v:
                w["end"] = float(v["end"])
            w["manual"] = v.get("conf", 0.9)
            n += 1
    return words, n


def monotonic(words, gap=0.02):''')

        if w.get("manual") is not None:
            w["conf"] = round(float(w["manual"]), 2)
    return words''')
s=s.replace('''    else:
        f = None
        words = plain_ends(words)
    words = monotonic(words)''','''    else:
        f = None
        words = plain_ends(words)

    words = monotonic(words)''')

            words, match, used = whisper_only(tokens, lines, ww)''','''        with T("map"):
            words, match, used = whisper_only(tokens, lines, ww)
            words, dbg["manual_fixes"] = apply_fixes(words, args.fix)
            for w in words:
                if w.get("manual") is not None:
                    w["conf"] = w["manual"]
            monotonic(words)''')

BashAdd whisper-only start cap heuristic and re-eval11:46 PM
input
description Add whisper-only start cap heuristic and re-eval
A=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align; python3 - <<'EOF'
p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/align.py'
s=open(p).read()
old='''    # enforce monotonic matches (drop matched tokens that go backwards in time)'''
new='''    # Whisper glues the silence before a line onto its first word (start is
    # typically 0.2-0.6 s early): cap a line-initial word's duration at ~1.25x
    # a nominal 0.2 s/syllable and move its start, keeping Whisper's end.
    for t in tokens:
        if t.get("src") == "whisper" and t["ti"] == 0:
            est = 0.2 * max(1, len(t["syl"])) + 0.05
            if t["end"] - t["start"] > 1.5 * est:
                t["start"] = t["end"] - 1.25 * est
                t["start_rule"] = "whisper-cap"
    # enforce monotonic matches (drop matched tokens that go backwards in time)'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new,1)
s=s.replace('''                for k in ("start", "end", "conf", "whisper", "src"):''','''                for k in ("start", "end", "conf", "whisper", "src", "start_rule"):''')
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

BashFix kick bias, regression grid fit, re-evaluate11:47 PM
input
description Fix kick bias, regression grid fit, re-evaluate
A=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align; python3 - <<'EOF'
p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/analyze.py'
s=open(p).read()
old='''    e = np.convolve(xb.astype(np.float64) ** 2, np.ones(w) / w, mode="same")[::h]'''
new='''    # trailing window: the log-energy rise starts AT the attack (a centred
    # window would report it w/2 early)
    e = np.convolve(xb.astype(np.float64) ** 2, np.ones(w) / w, mode="full")[:len(xb)][::h]'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
old='''    # phase refinement on kick attacks
    if len(kicks):
        n_ = np.round((kicks - off) / P)
        res = kicks - (off + n_ * P)
        res = res[np.abs(res) < 0.06]
        if len(res) >= 8:
            off += float(np.median(res))
            info["kick_residual_ms"] = round(float(np.std(res)) * 1000, 2)'''
new='''    # refine period + phase on kick attacks that sit on the grid (least squares)
    if len(kicks):
        for _ in range(2):
            n_ = np.round((kicks - off) / P)
            res = kicks - (off + n_ * P)
            ok = np.abs(res) < 0.05
            if ok.sum() < 16:
                break
            P_new, off_new = np.polyfit(n_[ok], kicks[ok], 1)
            if abs(P_new / P - 1) > 0.002:
                break
            P, off = float(P_new), float(off_new)
            info["kick_residual_ms"] = round(float(np.std(res[ok])) * 1000, 2)
            info["kick_fit_n"] = int(ok.sum())'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
old='''        if t - bounds[-1] >= min_bars * bar - 1e-3 and duration - t >= min_bars * bar * 0.5:'''
new='''        if t - bounds[-1] >= (min_bars - 0.5) * bar and duration - t >= min_bars * bar * 0.5:'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

// Beat grid helpers built from data/audio.json.
//   beatPos(t)   -> { beat (fractional index), i, phase, bar, barPhase, beatInBar }
//   beatTime(fi) -> seconds at a fractional beat index (extrapolates past the grid)
//   snap(t, mode) with mode 'beat' | 'half' | 'downbeat' | 'none'
//   kickEnv / sinceKick / hatEnv for strobes; sectionAt(t)

import { bisect, clamp } from './util.js';

export function makeBeats(audio) {
  const bpm = audio.bpm || 120;
  const beatDur = 60 / bpm;
  const bpb = audio.beats_per_bar || 4;
  const beats = (audio.beats && audio.beats.length ? audio.beats : [0]).slice();
  const downbeats = audio.downbeats && audio.downbeats.length ? audio.downbeats.slice() : beats.filter((_, i) => i % bpb === 0);
  const kicks = (audio.kicks || []).slice().sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const hats = (audio.hats && audio.hats.length ? audio.hats : beats.map((b) => b + beatDur / 2)).slice().sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const sections = (audio.sections || []).slice();
  const duration = audio.duration || (beats.at(-1) + beatDur);
  // index of the first downbeat in the beat array, so bar numbers follow the downbeats
  const db0 = Math.max(0, beats.findIndex((b) => Math.abs(b - downbeats[0]) < beatDur / 4));

"""Evaluate lyric alignment (and the beat grid) against the TTS test songs.

  uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/tools/eval_alignment.py [--run] [--md]

Test sets = every shared/align/test/vocal*/ with a truth.json (made by
tools/make_test_vocal.py; test/vocal = synth-demo backing, test/vocal_loop =
aevalsrc kick/hat/pad loop).  Results go to test/out/<set>/<config>/.

--run    (re)runs align.py in every configuration (and analyze.py) on each set
         (cached stems / emissions / whisper are reused unless --force)
--md     writes shared/align/EVAL.md

Metrics: absolute error of line starts (first word start vs. the first audible
sample of the TTS line), line ends, and the start of the backing-vocal part
inside a line (e.g. "(I'm crying)", rendered in a second voice): mean / median
/ 95th percentile / max in ms; also the same for lead lines only (lines that
are not entirely parenthesised backing vocals).
"""
import argparse
import json
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))
import common  # noqa: E402
import numpy as np  # noqa: E402

ROOT = common.ROOT
TESTS = ROOT / "test"
OUT = ROOT / "test" / "out"
CONFIGS = [  # label, input, flags, tag
    ("whisper-only", "mix.wav", ["--whisper-only"], "whisper_wav"),
    ("no-demucs", "mix.wav", ["--no-demucs"], "nodemucs_wav"),
    ("full + Demucs", "mix.wav", [], "full_wav"),
    ("full + Demucs, mp3 input", "mix.mp3", [], "full_mp3"),
]


def sh(cmd, log):
    t0 = time.time()
    with open(log, "w") as f:
        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    return time.time() - t0


def run_align(test, out, cfg, force=False, plots=False):
    label, inp, flags, tag = cfg
    cmd = ["uv", "run", "--project", str(ROOT), "python", str(ROOT / "align.py"), "--audio", str(test / inp),
           "--lyrics", str(test / "lyrics.txt"), "--out", str(out / tag)] + flags
    cmd += ["--force"] if force else []
    cmd += ["--plots"] if plots and tag == "full_wav" else []
    return sh(cmd, out / f"{tag}.log")


def run_analyze(test, out, force=False):
    cmd = ["uv", "run", "--project", str(ROOT), "python", str(ROOT / "analyze.py"), "--audio", str(test / "mix.wav"),
           "--out", str(out / "analyze"), "--plots"] + (["--force"] if force else [])
    return sh(cmd, out / "analyze.log")


def stats(err_s):
    e = np.abs(np.asarray(err_s)) * 1000
    if len(e) == 0:
        return dict(n=0, mean=float("nan"), median=float("nan"), p95=float("nan"), max=float("nan"), within100=0)
    return dict(n=len(e), mean=float(e.mean()), median=float(np.median(e)), p95=float(np.percentile(e, 95)),
                max=float(e.max()), within100=float(np.mean(e <= 100) * 100))


def evaluate(truth, lyr):
    L = {ln["i"]: ln for ln in lyr["lines"]}
    s_err, s_lead, e_err, bv_err, worst = [], [], [], [], []
    for g in truth["lines"]:
        p = L[g["i"]]
        d = p["start"] - g["start"]
        s_err.append(d)
        if not all(w.get("bv") for w in p["words"]):
            s_lead.append(d)
        e_err.append(p["end"] - g["end"])
        worst.append((abs(d), g["i"], g["text"], d))
        if len(g["runs"]) > 1:  # start of each later run (backing-vocal part)
            k = 0
            for ri, r in enumerate(g["runs"]):
                if ri > 0:
                    bv_err.append(p["words"][k]["start"] - r["start"])
                k += len(r["text"].split())
    worst.sort(reverse=True)
    return dict(start=stats(s_err), lead=stats(s_lead), end=stats(e_err), bv_start=stats(bv_err),
                signed_start_mean_ms=float(np.mean(s_err) * 1000), worst=worst[:5])


def eval_beats(truth, out):
    p = out / "analyze" / "audio.json"
    if not p.exists():
        return None
    a = json.loads(p.read_text())
    dbg = json.loads((out / "analyze" / "audio_debug.json").read_text())

    def hit(gt, pr):
        gt = np.asarray(gt)
        gt = gt[(gt > 1.0) & (gt < truth["duration"] - 1.0)]
        pr = np.asarray(pr)
        d = np.array([np.min(np.abs(pr - b)) for b in gt]) * 1000 if len(pr) else np.full(len(gt), np.inf)
        return d
    db = hit(truth["beats"], a["beats"])
    dd = hit(truth["downbeats"], a["downbeats"])
    return dict(bpm=a["bpm"], bpm_true=truth["bpm"], beat_err_mean_ms=float(db.mean()),
                beat_hit_rate_50ms=float(np.mean(db <= 50) * 100), downbeat_hit_rate_50ms=float(np.mean(dd <= 50) * 100),
                n_beats=len(a["beats"]), sections=[s["start"] for s in a["sections"]], n_kicks=len(a["kicks"]),
                timings=dbg.get("timings_s", {}))


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--run", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--force", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--plots", action="store_true", help="also write QA plots for the full_wav runs")
    ap.add_argument("--md", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--only", nargs="*", help="config tags to (re)run")
    ap.add_argument("--sets", nargs="*", help="test set dir names (default: all test/vocal*/)")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    sets = [TESTS / s for s in args.sets] if args.sets else sorted(p.parent for p in TESTS.glob("vocal*/truth.json"))
    results = []
    for test in sets:
        truth = json.loads((test / "truth.json").read_text())
        out = OUT / test.name
        out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        rows = []
        print(f"== {test.name}: backing {truth['backing']}")
        for cfg in CONFIGS:
            label, inp, flags, tag = cfg
            if args.run and (not args.only or tag in args.only):
                run_align(test, out, cfg, args.force, args.plots)
            p = out / tag / "lyrics.json"
            if not p.exists():
                print("   missing", p)
                continue
            dbg = json.loads((out / tag / "align_debug.json").read_text())
            r = evaluate(truth, json.loads(p.read_text()))
            r.update(label=label, tag=tag, timings=dbg.get("timings_s", {}))
            rows.append(r)
            s, ld = r["start"], r["lead"]
            print(f"   {label:26s} start mean {s['mean']:6.1f} med {s['median']:5.1f} p95 {s['p95']:6.1f} "
                  f"max {s['max']:7.1f} | lead mean {ld['mean']:6.1f} p95 {ld['p95']:6.1f} | end mean "
                  f"{r['end']['mean']:6.1f} | bv-part {r['bv_start']['mean']:6.1f} | signed {r['signed_start_mean_ms']:+.1f}")
        if args.run and (not args.only or "analyze" in args.only):
            run_analyze(test, out, args.force)
        beats = eval_beats(truth, out)
        if beats:
            print("   analyze.py:", {k: v for k, v in beats.items() if k != "sections"})
        results.append(dict(set=test.name, truth={k: truth[k] for k in truth if k not in ("lines", "beats", "downbeats")},
                            n_lines=len(truth["lines"]), rows=rows, beats=beats))
    (OUT / "eval_results.json").write_text(json.dumps(results, indent=1, default=str))
    if args.md:
        write_md(results)


def write_md(results):
    def f(x):
        return "n/a" if x != x else f"{x:.0f}"
    parts = []
    for res in results:
        tr = res["truth"]
        t = ["| configuration | line-start mean | median | p95 | max | ≤100 ms | lead lines mean / p95 / max "
             "| line-end mean | BV-part start mean / p95 |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
        for r in res["rows"]:
            s, ld, e, b = r["start"], r["lead"], r["end"], r["bv_start"]
            t.append(f"| {r['label']} | **{f(s['mean'])} ms** | {f(s['median'])} | {f(s['p95'])} | {f(s['max'])} | "
                     f"{s['within100']:.0f}% | {f(ld['mean'])} / {f(ld['p95'])} / {f(ld['max'])} | {f(e['mean'])} | "
                     f"{f(b['mean'])} / {f(b['p95'])} |")
        worst = [f"- {r['label']}: " + "; ".join(f"L{w[1]} `{w[2][:48]}` {w[3] * 1000:+.0f} ms" for w in r["worst"][:3])
                 for r in res["rows"]]
        bt = ""
        if res["beats"]:
            b = res["beats"]
            bt = (f"\nanalyze.py (no `--sections`): {b['bpm']} BPM (truth {b['bpm_true']:g}); "
                  f"{b['beat_hit_rate_50ms']:.0f}% of true beats have a detected beat within 50 ms (mean distance "
                  f"{b['beat_err_mean_ms']:.1f} ms); {b['downbeat_hit_rate_50ms']:.0f}% of true downbeats hit; "
                  f"{b['n_kicks']} kicks; section starts {', '.join(f'{x:g}' for x in b['sections'])}.\n")
        parts.append(f"""### `test/{res['set']}`: backing = {tr['backing']}

{res['n_lines']} lines, {tr['duration']:.0f} s, vocal {tr['vocal_db']:+g} dB vs. backing RMS.

{chr(10).join(t)}

Worst lines:
{chr(10).join(worst)}
{bt}""")
    md = """# Alignment evaluation

Generated by `uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/tools/eval_alignment.py --run --md`.

**Test songs** (`tools/make_test_vocal.py`): every line of `songs/2am-crying/suno_lyrics_v1.txt` (snapshot in
`test/vocal*/lyrics.txt`) is rendered separately with macOS `say` (lead voice Samantha, the parenthesised backing
vocals in Daniel, 185 wpm), silence-trimmed, and placed on the 128 BPM beat grid with known gaps, so the ground
truth of every line start / end is exact to the sample.  The vocal is mixed 3 dB below the backing RMS.
"BV-part start" = start of the second-voice part inside a line, i.e. the first word of `(I'm crying)`: the only
word-level ground truth available.  "Lead lines" excludes the four lines that are entirely backing-vocal
non-words (`(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)`, `(untz.)`).

""" + "\n".join(parts) + """
## Reading the numbers

- **Full pipeline with Demucs meets the < 150 ms target**: ~50 ms mean over all lines, ~17 ms median, and every
  lead line within ~60 ms.  The mean is dominated by one line: `(untz.)`, the last line of the sheet, which the
  synth backing also "sings" (its formant `untz` one-shot and baby-cry lead are voice-like, so Demucs keeps them
  in the vocal stem); CTC then has two equally good candidates and picks the synth's.  On the loop backing,
  which has no voice-like content, that error disappears.
- **mp3 vs. wav**: the same mix encoded with libmp3lame (LAME tag, 576-sample encoder delay) gives the same
  line starts within a few ms, i.e. the gapless decode keeps the timeline intact.
- **no-demucs** is ~2x worse on average and noticeably worse at the tails, but still usable on this easy material.
- **whisper-only** is a coarse preview: Whisper glues the silence before a line onto its first word, so line
  starts come out early (a duration cap on line-initial words removes part of the bias), and lines Whisper
  does not transcribe (non-words, repeated backing vocals) are interpolated.

## Caveats (read before trusting these numbers)

- Spoken TTS is much easier than a sung Suno vocal: clean consonant onsets, no melisma, no held vowels, no
  reverb tails, no doubled / harmonised lead, no ad-libs.  Expect real songs to be noticeably worse, mainly on
  long legato notes, buried choruses and ad-lib-heavy sections; check the `--plots` QA images and use `--fix`.
- The ground truth is line level only (plus the start of each backing-vocal part).  Word-level accuracy inside
  a line is not measured; `conf` (model agreement + CTC posterior + Whisper agreement) is the per-word signal.
- Both backings are synthetic; masking in a real mix (guitars, pads, reverb, sidechain) is different.
"""
    (ROOT / "EVAL.md").write_text(md)
    print("wrote", ROOT / "EVAL.md")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

cat > /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/README.md <<'EOF'
# shared/align — lyric timings and beat grid for the renderer

Given a finished song (mp3/wav from Suno) and its Suno lyric sheet, produce

- `lyrics.json` — word-level lyric timings (+ syllables for multi-syllable words, backing-vocal flags, confidence)
- `audio.json` — bpm, beats, downbeats, sections, kicks, vocal onsets, loudness envelope

for the browser renderer (`shared/renderer/`).  Mirrors the `analysis/` directory of
[mexicat/pdoom-video](https://github.com/mexicat/pdoom-video), automated (no per-song hand-tuned anchors; manual
corrections go in a `--fix` JSON instead of the source).

## Install

Needs [uv](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/) and `ffmpeg` on PATH (Apple Silicon Mac; Whisper runs on MLX).

```sh
uv sync --project shared/align                                   # Python 3.12 (pinned; torch has no 3.14 wheels yet)
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/tools/prefetch_models.py   # optional: download all weights now
```

All commands below run from the repo root with absolute or repo-relative paths; nothing needs a `cd`.

## The three commands

```sh
# 1. full alignment (Demucs vocal stem + CTC forced alignment + refinement + Whisper cross-check)
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/align.py \
    --audio songs/2am-crying/audio/<file>.mp3 \
    --lyrics songs/2am-crying/suno_lyrics_v1.txt \
    --out songs/2am-crying/data --plots

# 2. fast coarse preview: Whisper word timestamps on the mix mapped onto the sheet
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/align.py --whisper-only \
    --audio songs/2am-crying/audio/<file>.mp3 --lyrics songs/2am-crying/suno_lyrics_v1.txt \
    --out songs/2am-crying/data

# 3. beat grid / sections / kicks / loudness
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/analyze.py \
    --audio songs/2am-crying/audio/<file>.mp3 --out songs/2am-crying/data \
    [--sections songs/2am-crying/synth/out/song.json]
```

`align.py` flags: `--no-demucs` (CTC on the mix: no separation, no signal refinement), `--whisper-only`,
`--plots` (one PNG per line in `<out>/qa/line_NN.png`: vocal waveform, spectrogram, final word boundaries,
the two CTC models' boundaries and Whisper's words; these are the QA artefact to look at), `--pron pron.json`
(spoken spellings for odd tokens, e.g. `{"Piaget's": "pee ah zhays", "Nanit": "nan it"}`), `--fix fixes.json`
(manual corrections after looking at the plots: `{"12:3": {"start": 45.21}, "40": {"shift": -0.05}}`, line:word
0-based as in lyrics.json; fixed words get conf 0.9 unless given), `--no-whisper`, `--force` (ignore caches),
`--gap-penalty`, `--margin` (Viterbi tuning, see `ctc.py`).

`align.py` also writes `<out>/align_debug.json`: sheet section per line, per-word CTC spans per unit, the
refinement rule that set each start, Whisper match, confidence parts, stem lag, per-stage timings.

`analyze.py` flags: `--sections song.json` (take bpm / beat grid / downbeats / sections from a known structure
file as the truth; only kicks, vocal onsets and loudness are computed; keys are read tolerantly:
`bpm|tempo`, `beats` (floats or `{t}`), `downbeats` or every `beats_per_bar`-th beat, `sections` with
`name|label` and `start|start_s` / `end|end_s`; the synth's `song.json` works as is), `--no-demucs` (kicks from
the mix, no vocal onsets), `--k N` (number of section clusters; default from the eigengap), `--plots`.
It also writes `<out>/audio_debug.json` (grid fit, downbeat phase scores, segmentation labels, timings).

## Running it on a real Suno download

1. Put the file at `songs/<song>/audio/<file>.mp3` (audio is gitignored; keep the original mp3: the browser
   plays the same file, so the timeline matches).
2. Run commands 1 and 3 with `--out songs/<song>/data` (and `--plots`).  Use the same `--lyrics` sheet you gave
   Suno; if Suno sang something different (it often repeats a chorus, drops an ad-lib or adds one), edit a copy
   of the sheet to what is actually sung first, since every sheet line is forced onto the audio.
3. Open `songs/<song>/data/qa/line_*.png`, sorted by the words with low `conf` (listed in the notes /
   `align_debug.json`).  Correct what is wrong in a `songs/<song>/data/fixes.json` and rerun command 1 with
   `--fix songs/<song>/data/fixes.json` (cached stems / emissions make the rerun take seconds).
4. Commit `lyrics.json` and `audio.json` (and `fixes.json`); the debug JSON and plots are optional.

## Outputs

`lyrics.json` (times in s, 3 decimals; conf 2 decimals):
`{lines: [{i, text, start, end, words: [{w, start, end, conf, syl?, bv?}]}], extras: [{start, end, desc}], notes}`.
`w` is the display token from the sheet (punctuation and parentheses kept; `" ".join(w) == text`).
`syl` = `[[start, end], ...]` per syllable, only for words with more than one syllable (orthographic syllables
timed by the CTC character path; tokens like `2am` / `5:40` / `BPM` split into their spoken parts).
`bv: true` only on backing-vocal words (parenthesised on the sheet).  `extras` = vocal activity (vocal stem) or
Whisper words not covered by any lyric word (ad-libs, unlisted backing vocals), with Whisper's text when it heard
something.  Sheet section headers are not in lyrics.json (no slot in the schema); they are per line in
`align_debug.json`.

`audio.json`: `{bpm, beats, downbeats, sections: [{name, start, end}], kicks, vocal_onsets,
loudness: {hop: 0.01, rms_db}, duration}`.  Detected sections are named `s1, s2, ...`.

**Timeline**: every time is on the gapless ffmpeg decode of the input file.  For mp3s with a LAME/Xing tag
(Suno's and ffmpeg's), ffmpeg trims the encoder delay + decoder delay exactly like Chrome / Firefox do, so the
times match browser playback.  Demucs is run through its Python API on that same decode, so the stems need no
shift (pdoom-video's -23 ms shift came from the demucs CLI decoding the mp3 itself); `stems.py` still measures the
stem-vs-mix lag by cross-correlation and logs it in the debug JSON (0 samples on the test songs).

## How it works

See the docstrings at the top of `align.py`, `ctc.py`, `stems.py`, `analyze.py`.  Short version:
gapless decode → Demucs `htdemucs_ft` vocal stem → CTC emissions (20 ms) from torchaudio `MMS_FA` and
`WAV2VEC2_ASR_LARGE_LV60K_960H` (mono stem, plus L/R channels when the stem is really stereo), fused → one
constrained Viterbi forced alignment over all lines, with a garbage "star" token between lines to absorb
ad-libs, and a small per-frame penalty on blanks inside a line so a line stays compact → per-unit signal
refinement on the vocal stem (voice re-entry after ≥ 50 ms silence, snap to a spectral-flux onset, pull
fricatives back to the start of their noise; ends at the next start when legato, else where the voice drops
15 dB) → monotonic cleanup (starts increase by ≥ 20 ms) → mlx-whisper `large-v3-turbo` word timestamps matched
to the sheet with difflib `SequenceMatcher` on normalised units (matches > 1.5 s off rejected) → conf =
0.35 + 0.30 × model agreement (≤ 60 ms) + 0.20 × CTC posterior + 0.15 × Whisper agreement (≤ 150 ms).

Text normalisation (`common.units`) is shared by the CTC targets and the Whisper matching: `2am` → two ay em,
`5:40` → five forty, `10:45` → ten forty five, `a.m.` → ay em, `BPM` → bee pee em, hyphenated words and numbers
split, all-caps acronyms without vowels spelled out.  Override anything odd with `--pron`.

## Testing

```sh
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/tools/make_test_vocal.py                    # test/vocal (synth backing if present)
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/tools/make_test_vocal.py --backing loop --out shared/align/test/vocal_loop
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/tools/eval_alignment.py --run --md          # -> EVAL.md
```

`make_test_vocal.py` renders every sheet line with macOS `say` (Samantha; backing vocals in Daniel), places the
lines on the 128 BPM grid with known gaps, mixes them under the synth demo (`songs/2am-crying/synth/out/*.wav`,
if present) or an ffmpeg `aevalsrc` kick + hat (+ pad) loop, and writes `mix.wav`, `mix.mp3`, `truth.json`
(exact line starts / ends, beat grid).  Results and caveats: [EVAL.md](EVAL.md).

BashIntegrate optional karaoke lead stem, test it11:50 PM
input
description Integrate optional karaoke lead stem, test it
run_in_background true
A=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align; cat >> $A/stems.py <<'EOF'


KARAOKE_MODEL = "mel_band_roformer_karaoke_aufr33_viperx_sdr_10.1956.ckpt"


def separate_lead(audio_path, y=None, force=False):
    """Lead vocal via the mel-band-roformer karaoke model (audio-separator,
    optional extra: `uv sync --project shared/align --extra karaoke`).
    Runs on a float WAV of our gapless decode, so it shares the timeline.
    Returns ((2, n) lead stem, info)."""
    import soundfile as sf
    wd = common.work_dir(audio_path) / "stems"
    wd.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    lead_p, info_p = wd / "lead.wav", wd / "lead_info.json"
    if lead_p.exists() and info_p.exists() and not force:
        info = json.loads(info_p.read_text())
        info["cached"] = True
        return sf.read(lead_p, dtype="float32", always_2d=True)[0].T.copy(), info
    try:
        from audio_separator.separator import Separator
    except ImportError as e:
        raise SystemExit("--karaoke needs the optional extra: uv sync --project shared/align --extra karaoke") from e
    if y is None:
        y = common.decode(audio_path)
    tmp = wd / "karaoke_tmp"
    tmp.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    src = tmp / "input.wav"
    sf.write(src, y.T, common.SR, subtype="FLOAT")
    sep = Separator(model_file_dir=str(common.CACHE / "audio-separator"), output_dir=str(tmp), output_format="WAV")
    sep.load_model(model_filename=KARAOKE_MODEL)
    outs = sep.separate(str(src))
    voc = next(tmp / o for o in outs if "(Vocals)" in o)
    ins = next(tmp / o for o in outs if "(Instrumental)" in o)
    lead = sf.read(voc, dtype="float32", always_2d=True)[0].T
    inst = sf.read(ins, dtype="float32", always_2d=True)[0].T
    n = y.shape[1]
    lead = np.pad(lead, ((0, 0), (0, max(0, n - lead.shape[1]))))[:, :n]
    inst = np.pad(inst, ((0, 0), (0, max(0, n - inst.shape[1]))))[:, :n]
    lag = measure_lag(y.mean(0), (lead + inst).mean(0), common.SR)
    if lag:
        lead = np.roll(lead, -lag, axis=1)
    sf.write(lead_p, lead.T, common.SR, subtype="FLOAT")
    for p in tmp.iterdir():
        p.unlink()
    tmp.rmdir()
    info = dict(model=KARAOKE_MODEL, lag_samples=int(lag))
    info_p.write_text(json.dumps(info))
    info["cached"] = False
    return lead, info
EOF

description Update README for karaoke default and substitutions
A=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align; python3 - <<'EOF'
p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/README.md'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace('''# 1. full alignment (Demucs vocal stem + CTC forced alignment + refinement + Whisper cross-check)''','''# 1. full alignment (Demucs + karaoke vocal stems, CTC forced alignment, refinement, Whisper cross-check)''')
s=s.replace('''`align.py` flags: `--no-demucs` (CTC on the mix: no separation, no signal refinement), `--whisper-only`,''','''`align.py` flags: `--no-demucs` (CTC on the mix: no separation, no signal refinement), `--no-karaoke` (full
mode without the mel-roformer karaoke lead-vocal stem: ~80 s faster, but less robust when the backing has
voice-like parts or stacked backing vocals), `--whisper-only`,''')
s=s.replace('''gapless decode → Demucs `htdemucs_ft` vocal stem → CTC emissions (20 ms) from torchaudio `MMS_FA` and
`WAV2VEC2_ASR_LARGE_LV60K_960H` (mono stem, plus L/R channels when the stem is really stereo), fused → one''','''gapless decode → Demucs `htdemucs_ft` vocal stem + mel-band-roformer karaoke lead-vocal stem
(audio-separator) → CTC emissions (20 ms) from torchaudio `MMS_FA` and `WAV2VEC2_ASR_LARGE_LV60K_960H` (Demucs
mono stem, karaoke lead, plus L/R channels when the stem is really stereo), fused → one''')
subst = '''- **Nothing had to fall back**: torchaudio `MMS_FA`, `WAV2VEC2_ASR_LARGE_LV60K_960H`, Demucs `htdemucs_ft`
  (on MPS, CPU fallback built in) and mlx-whisper `large-v3-turbo` all install and run on Python 3.12 /
  torch 2.14 / torchaudio 2.11 on this M4 Max.  torchaudio is used only for its model pipelines (the Viterbi is
  our own numba code, `torchaudio.functional.forced_align` and torchaudio I/O are not used).
- **audio-separator is in** (default dependency): it resolved cleanly against the same torch / numpy (it needs
  `onnxruntime` listed explicitly and pulls in torchvision).  The karaoke model
  `mel_band_roformer_karaoke_aufr33_viperx_sdr_10.1956.ckpt` (913 MB) gives a lead-vocal stem that is fused into
  the CTC emissions; on the synth-backed test song it removed the one large error (a backing-track "untz" that
  CTC preferred over the sung one).  `--no-karaoke` turns it off.
- pdoom-video's per-song `ANCHORS` / `FIX` dicts in the source are replaced by a `--fix` JSON file; there is no
  per-song tuning in the code.
- Additions over the reference: a per-frame penalty on blank frames *inside* a line in the Viterbi (keeps
  non-words like "untz" / "shh", which no CTC model recognises, from being smeared across the silence before a
  line), a guard so the fricative rule cannot walk back into silence, and the Whisper-only first-word duration cap.
- Not ported: pyin pitch features (only used for the reference's plots / legato pitch onsets), the snare / hat
  onset lists and the 100 fps band envelopes of pdoom's audio.json (not in this schema; the renderer derives
  hats from the beat grid).  `audio.json` has exactly the keys of the schema.
- Syllables are orthographic (pyphen hyphenation + a vowel-group heuristic), not phonetic: good enough to drive
  a per-syllable wipe, occasionally off by a letter at a cluster boundary.
- Commit trailer: commits use the `Co-Authored-By` line from the harness attribution settings.'''
s=s.replace('SUBST_PLACEHOLDER', subst)
open(p,'w').write(s)

BashFix beat phase via kicks and downbeat prior; re-eval12:00 AM
input
description Fix beat phase via kicks and downbeat prior; re-eval
A=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align; python3 - <<'EOF'
p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/analyze.py'
s=open(p).read()
old='''    _, P, off = best
    # refine period + phase on kick attacks that sit on the grid (least squares)'''
new='''    _, P, off = best
    # beat phase from the kicks: spectral flux likes off-beat hats as much as
    # kicks, but kicks sit on the beat in nearly every style.  If the kicks are
    # concentrated at one phase of the grid, move the grid there.
    if len(kicks) >= 16:
        ph = np.exp(2j * np.pi * (kicks - off) / P)
        R, mu = float(np.abs(ph.mean())), float(np.angle(ph.mean()) / (2 * np.pi))
        info["kick_phase"] = dict(R=round(R, 3), mu=round(mu, 3))
        if R > 0.3 and abs(mu) > 0.1:
            off += mu * P
            info["kick_phase"]["moved_s"] = round(mu * P, 4)
    # refine period + phase on kick attacks that sit on the grid (least squares)'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
old='''    ev = z(lo_b) + 1.5 * z(nov) - 0.5 * z(hi_b)
    scores = [float(ev[p::bpb].mean()) for p in range(bpb)]
    return int(np.argmax(scores)), scores'''
new='''    ev = z(lo_b) + 1.5 * z(nov) - 0.5 * z(hi_b)
    scores = [float(ev[p::bpb].mean()) for p in range(bpb)]
    # weak prior: the music starts on a downbeat (first beat with a real low-band
    # onset); decides only when the bar-level evidence is flat (e.g. a loop)
    strong = np.flatnonzero(lo_b > 0.5 * np.median(lo_b[lo_b > 0])) if np.any(lo_b > 0) else []
    if len(strong):
        scores[int(strong[0]) % bpb] += 0.1
    return int(np.argmax(scores)), scores'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

BashAllow first beat at zero; re-evaluate beats12:00 AM
input
description Allow first beat at zero; re-evaluate beats
A=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align; python3 - <<'EOF'
p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/analyze.py'
s=open(p).read()
old='''    off = off - P * math.floor(off / P)
    beats = off + P * np.arange(int((duration - off) / P) + 1)'''
new='''    # first beat: allow one a hair before 0 (a song that starts on the beat) and clip it to 0
    off = off - P * math.floor((off + 0.02) / P)
    beats = np.maximum(off + P * np.arange(int((duration - off) / P) + 1), 0.0)'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new); open(p,'w').write(s)

BashFix chroma novelty indexing; re-evaluate downbeats12:01 AM
input
description Fix chroma novelty indexing; re-evaluate downbeats
A=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align; python3 - <<'EOF'
p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/analyze.py'
s=open(p).read()
old='''    bf = np.clip(np.round(np.asarray(beats) * fps).astype(int), 0, ch.shape[1] - 1)
    sync = librosa.util.sync(ch, bf, aggregate=np.median)  # chroma per beat interval
    sync = sync / (np.linalg.norm(sync, axis=0, keepdims=True) + 1e-9)
    nov = np.zeros(len(bf))
    # chord change at beat k: compare the 2 beats before vs the 2 beats after
    for k in range(2, len(bf) - 2):
        a = sync[:, k - 1:k + 1].mean(1) if k < sync.shape[1] else sync[:, -1]
        b = sync[:, k + 1:k + 3].mean(1) if k + 3 <= sync.shape[1] else sync[:, -1]
        nov[k] = 1 - float(a @ b / (np.linalg.norm(a) * np.linalg.norm(b) + 1e-9))'''
new='''    bf = np.clip(np.round(np.asarray(beats) * fps).astype(int), 0, ch.shape[1] - 1)
    # chroma of the interval that starts at beat k (column k), median-aggregated
    edges = list(bf) + [ch.shape[1]]
    C = np.stack([np.median(ch[:, a:max(b, a + 1)], axis=1) for a, b in zip(edges[:-1], edges[1:])], axis=1)
    C = C / (np.linalg.norm(C, axis=0, keepdims=True) + 1e-9)
    nov = np.zeros(len(bf))
    # chord change at beat k: the 2 beats before (k-2, k-1) vs the 2 beats from k on (k, k+1)
    for k in range(2, len(bf) - 1):
        a = C[:, k - 2:k].mean(1)
        b = C[:, k:k + 2].mean(1)
        nov[k] = 1 - float(a @ b / (np.linalg.norm(a) * np.linalg.norm(b) + 1e-9))'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new); open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/align.py'
s=open(p).read()
old='''        end = min(q, nxt) if q is not None else nxt
        end = max(end, w["start"] + 0.04)'''
new='''        end = min(q, nxt) if q is not None else nxt
        end = min(end, e + MAX_HOLD)  # runaway guard (voice-like backing after the last word)
        end = max(end, w["start"] + 0.04)'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
old='''FEAT_SR = 24000'''
new='''MAX_HOLD = 2.5  # s a word may extend past its CTC span (held notes)
FEAT_SR = 24000'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)

# Alignment evaluation

Generated by `uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/tools/eval_alignment.py --run --md`.

**Test songs** (`tools/make_test_vocal.py`): every line of `songs/2am-crying/suno_lyrics_v1.txt` (snapshot in
`test/vocal*/lyrics.txt`) is rendered separately with macOS `say` (lead voice Samantha, the parenthesised backing
vocals in Daniel, 185 wpm), silence-trimmed, and placed on the 128 BPM beat grid with known gaps, so the ground
truth of every line start / end is exact to the sample.  The vocal is mixed 3 dB below the backing RMS.
"BV-part start" = start of the second-voice part inside a line, i.e. the first word of `(I'm crying)`: the only
word-level ground truth available.  "Lead lines" excludes the four lines that are entirely backing-vocal
non-words (`(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)`, `(untz.)`).

### `test/vocal`: backing = synth:songs/2am-crying/synth/out/2am_crying.wav

48 lines, 180 s, vocal -3 dB vs. backing RMS.

| configuration | line-start mean | median | p95 | max | ≤100 ms | lead lines mean / p95 / max | line-end mean | BV-part start mean / p95 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| whisper-only | **248 ms** | 169 | 813 | 1513 | 17% | 190 / 451 / 571 | 297 | 639 / 801 |
| no-demucs | **91 ms** | 29 | 94 | 1919 | 96% | 27 / 67 / 99 | 169 | 66 / 82 |
| full, Demucs only (--no-karaoke) | **50 ms** | 16 | 34 | 1589 | 98% | 18 / 34 / 56 | 124 | 16 / 41 |
| full (Demucs + karaoke lead) | **19 ms** | 19 | 39 | 79 | 100% | 18 / 34 / 56 | 132 | 16 / 41 |
| full, mp3 input | **20 ms** | 21 | 34 | 79 | 100% | 18 / 34 / 34 | 87 | 25 / 48 |

Worst lines:
- whisper-only: L16 `(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)` -1513 ms; L47 `(untz.)` -981 ms; L1 `(untz. untz. untz. untz.)` -942 ms
- no-demucs: L47 `(untz.)` +1919 ms; L16 `(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)` -1162 ms; L11 `I can't reach it. You can reach it. Here's how I` +99 ms
- full, Demucs only (--no-karaoke): L47 `(untz.)` +1589 ms; L36 `The group chat gets its post: "Baby asleep, 10:4` -56 ms; L12 `It's 2am and I'm crying (I'm crying)` +34 ms
- full (Demucs + karaoke lead): L47 `(untz.)` +79 ms; L36 `The group chat gets its post: "Baby asleep, 10:4` -56 ms; L17 `(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)` +42 ms
- full, mp3 input: L47 `(untz.)` +79 ms; L17 `(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)` +42 ms; L12 `It's 2am and I'm crying (I'm crying)` +34 ms

analyze.py (no `--sections`): 127.999 BPM (truth 128); 100% of true beats have a detected beat within 50 ms (mean distance 1.9 ms); 100% of true downbeats hit; 305 kicks; section starts 0, 7.498, 14.998, 29.998, 44.998, 52.498, 82.498, 89.998, 104.998, 112.498, 142.498, 157.498.

| full (Demucs + karaoke lead) | **15 ms** | 11 | 32 | 42 | 100% | 14 / 32 / 34 | 54 | 10 / 35 |

Worst lines:
- whisper-only: L16 `(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)` -1526 ms; L22 `Ferber says to check at three, then five, then t` -1096 ms; L1 `(untz. untz. untz. untz.)` -942 ms
- no-demucs: L11 `I can't reach it. You can reach it. Here's how I` +99 ms; L10 `I dropped the paci through the slats. It's under` +86 ms; L5 `I've got object permanence now. That's Piaget's ` +69 ms
- full, Demucs only (--no-karaoke): L12 `It's 2am and I'm crying (I'm crying)` +34 ms; L18 `Four a.m., you Google "is this normal" with the ` +32 ms; L8 `Night-vision on the monitor, little meerkat in t` +32 ms
- full (Demucs + karaoke lead): L17 `(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)` +42 ms; L12 `It's 2am and I'm crying (I'm crying)` +34 ms; L18 `Four a.m., you Google "is this normal" with the ` +32 ms
- full, mp3 input: L12 `It's 2am and I'm crying (I'm crying)` +34 ms; L44 `It's 5:40 and... good morning!` +34 ms; L18 `Four a.m., you Google "is this normal" with the ` +32 ms

analyze.py (no `--sections`): 128.0 BPM (truth 128); 100% of true beats have a detected beat within 50 ms (mean distance 0.3 ms); 0% of true downbeats hit; 384 kicks; section starts 0, 9.844, 19.219, 39.844, 49.219, 60.469, 71.719, 107.344, 116.719, 124.219, 131.718, 154.218, 165.468, 172.968.

## Reading the numbers

- **Full pipeline with Demucs meets the < 150 ms target**: ~50 ms mean over all lines, ~17 ms median, and every
  lead line within ~60 ms.  The mean is dominated by one line: `(untz.)`, the last line of the sheet, which the
  synth backing also "sings" (its formant `untz` one-shot and baby-cry lead are voice-like, so Demucs keeps them
  in the vocal stem); CTC then has two equally good candidates and picks the synth's.  On the loop backing,
  which has no voice-like content, that error disappears.
- **mp3 vs. wav**: the same mix encoded with libmp3lame (LAME tag, 576-sample encoder delay) gives the same
  line starts within a few ms, i.e. the gapless decode keeps the timeline intact.
- **no-demucs** is ~2x worse on average and noticeably worse at the tails, but still usable on this easy material.
- **whisper-only** is a coarse preview: Whisper glues the silence before a line onto its first word, so line
  starts come out early (a duration cap on line-initial words removes part of the bias), and lines Whisper
  does not transcribe (non-words, repeated backing vocals) are interpolated.

## Caveats (read before trusting these numbers)

- Spoken TTS is much easier than a sung Suno vocal: clean consonant onsets, no melisma, no held vowels, no
  reverb tails, no doubled / harmonised lead, no ad-libs.  Expect real songs to be noticeably worse, mainly on
  long legato notes, buried choruses and ad-lib-heavy sections; check the `--plots` QA images and use `--fix`.
- The ground truth is line level only (plus the start of each backing-vocal part).  Word-level accuracy inside
  a line is not measured; `conf` (model agreement + CTC posterior + Whisper agreement) is the per-word signal.
- Both backings are synthetic; masking in a real mix (guitars, pads, reverb, sidechain) is different.

A=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align; python3 - <<'EOF'
p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/tools/eval_alignment.py'
s=open(p).read()
a=s.index('## Reading the numbers')
b=s.index('## Caveats (read before trusting these numbers)')
new='''## Reading the numbers (written for the 2026-10-17 run)

- **The default full pipeline (Demucs + karaoke lead stem) meets the < 150 ms target on both songs**: line-start
  error ~19 ms (synth backing) / ~15 ms (loop) mean, p95 <= 40 ms, worst line 79 ms.  The remaining error is
  mostly the 20 ms CTC frame grid plus TTS words that start with a soft consonant.
- **Why the karaoke stem is on by default**: the synth demo contains voice-like parts (a formant "untz" one-shot
  and a formant baby-cry lead singing the hook), which Demucs keeps in its vocal stem.  With Demucs alone the last
  line `(untz.)` was aligned to the synth's "untz" 1.6 s too late; the karaoke model separates the TTS lead from
  those parts and the miss disappears.  On the loop backing (nothing voice-like) both variants are equal.  It costs
  ~80-100 s per song and 0.9 GB of weights.
- **mp3 vs. wav**: the same mix encoded with libmp3lame (LAME tag, 576-sample encoder delay) gives line starts
  within ~1 ms (mean) of the wav run, i.e. the gapless decode keeps the timeline intact.
- **no-demucs** is about 2x worse than full on clean material (31 vs. 15 ms) and fails on the non-word
  backing-vocal lines when the backing has voice-like parts (up to 1.9 s), since there is no separation and no
  signal refinement.
- **whisper-only** is a coarse preview (~250 ms mean, early bias): Whisper glues the silence before a line onto its
  first word (a duration cap on line-initial words removes part of that bias), and words it does not transcribe
  (non-words, repeated backing vocals) are interpolated.
- **analyze.py**: tempo and beat phase are exact on both songs (beat error 0.3-2 ms).  Downbeats are right on the
  synth song (chord changes on the one); on the loop every bar is identical (one chord, same drum pattern), so
  nothing marks bar 1 and the bar phase is a guess, here off by one beat (0% hit).  Section starts on the synth
  song land on the real boundaries at 7.5, 52.5, 82.5, 112.5, 142.5 and 157.5 s, add sub-section splits at
  15 / 30 / 45 / 90 / 105 s and place the two build-ups (37.5, 97.5) 4-8 bars late; on the loop (no structure) the
  boundaries just follow the vocal gaps.

'''
s=s[:a]+new+s[b:]
open(p,'w').write(s)

A=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align; python3 - <<'EOF'

def rep(old, new, cnt=1):
    global s
    assert s.count(old) == cnt, (old, s.count(old))
    s = s.replace(old, new)
rep('''def build_tokens(lines):
    toks = []
    for ln in lines:
        for ti, w in enumerate(ln["words"]):
            us = common.units(w["w"])
            toks.append(dict(li=ln["i"], ti=ti, w=w["w"], bv=w["bv"], unit_texts=us,
                             syl=common.token_syllables(w["w"])))
    return toks''','''def build_tokens(lines):
    """All display tokens.  k = ordinal among the line's PRONOUNCEABLE tokens
    (None for tokens like a spaced dash or "..." that normalise to nothing:
    they are kept out of the alignment and re-inserted by insert_silent)."""
    toks = []
    for ln in lines:
        k = 0
        for ti, w in enumerate(ln["words"]):
            us = common.units(w["w"])
            toks.append(dict(li=ln["i"], ti=ti, k=k if us else None, w=w["w"], bv=w["bv"], unit_texts=us,
                             syl=common.token_syllables(w["w"])))
            k += bool(us)
    return toks


def insert_silent(words, all_tokens):
    """Re-insert unpronounceable tokens with zero duration next to their
    neighbour in the same line (previous word's end, or the next word's start
    when the token opens the line), conf 0.3."""
    by = {(w["li"], w["ti"]): w for w in words}
    out = []
    for t in all_tokens:
        if (t["li"], t["ti"]) in by:
            out.append(by[(t["li"], t["ti"])])
            continue
        prev = out[-1] if out and out[-1]["li"] == t["li"] else None
        nxt = next((by[(x["li"], x["ti"])] for x in all_tokens
                    if x["li"] == t["li"] and x["ti"] > t["ti"] and (x["li"], x["ti"]) in by), None)
        at = prev["end"] if prev is not None else nxt["start"]
        w = dict(t, start=at, end=at, conf=0.3, start_rule="silent-token", syl_t=None)
        out.append(w)
    return out''')
rep('''    tokens = build_tokens(lines)''','''    all_tokens = build_tokens(lines)
    tokens = [t for t in all_tokens if t["unit_texts"]]''')
rep('''            sp = spans[(t["li"], t["ti"])]''','''            sp = spans[(t["li"], t["k"])]''')
rep('''        write_outputs(out, lines, words, extras, notes, dbg, T, ww, mode)''','''        words = insert_silent(words, all_tokens)
        write_outputs(out, lines, words, extras, notes, dbg, T, ww, mode)''')
rep('''    write_outputs(out, lines, words, extras, notes, dbg, T, ww, mode)
    if args.plots:''','''    words = insert_silent(words, all_tokens)
    write_outputs(out, lines, words, extras, notes, dbg, T, ww, mode)
    if args.plots:''')
open(p,'w').write(s)
p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/common.py'

old='''    t = token.replace("’", "'").replace("‘", "'").replace("`", "'")'''
new='''    t = token.replace("’", "'").replace("‘", "'").replace("`", "'")
    # fold accents (café -> cafe, naïve -> naive) before anything strips non-ASCII
    t = "".join(c for c in unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", t) if not unicodedata.combining(c))'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
s=s.replace('''import time  # noqa: E402
''','''import time  # noqa: E402
