Opus: build lyric alignment pipeline final report12:11 AM

The pipeline is built and committed in /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/. On the test song, line-start error with Demucs is 15–20 ms on average, well under the 150 ms target. That test song is spoken text-to-speech (macOS say), not singing, so a real Suno vocal will do worse.

Results (EVAL.md)

Both test songs are 48 lines, 180 s, made by rendering each lyric line with say and placing it at known times. One is mixed under the synth demo WAV, the other under an ffmpeg kick/hat/pad loop. Line-start error in ms:

configurationsynth backing: mean / median / p95 / maxloop backing: mean / median / p95 / max
whisper-only248 / 169 / 813 / 1513256 / 171 / 821 / 1526
no-demucs91 / 29 / 94 / 191931 / 30 / 68 / 99
full, Demucs only (--no-karaoke)50 / 16 / 34 / 158915 / 11 / 32 / 34
full (default: Demucs + karaoke lead stem)19 / 19 / 39 / 7915 / 11 / 32 / 42
full, mp3 input20 / 21 / 34 / 7916 / 13 / 32 / 34
  • mp3 vs wav: the mp3 input lands within about 1 ms of the wav, so the encoder-delay handling works.
  • Why the karaoke stem is on by default: the synth demo has voice-like parts (a formant "untz" and a synth baby-cry singing the hook) that Demucs keeps in its vocal stem. With Demucs alone, the last line (untz.) locked onto the synth's "untz" 1.6 s late; the karaoke stem fixes that.
  • Whisper-only is a rough preview: line starts come out early because Whisper attaches the silence before a line to its first word.
  • analyze.py: tempo and beats are right on both songs (beat error 0.3–2 ms). Downbeats are 100% right on the synth song. On the loop they were 0%: every bar is identical, so nothing marks beat one.
  • Sections: on the synth song the detected boundaries hit 6 of the 8 real ones exactly, place the two build-ups 4 bars late, and add a few extra splits.
  • --sections: tested with the synth's real song.json and with a small fixture in test/fixtures/.

Commands (from the repo root)

uv sync --project shared/align
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/tools/prefetch_models.py
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/align.py --audio songs/<song>/audio/<file>.mp3 --lyrics songs/<song>/suno_lyrics_v1.txt --out songs/<song>/data --plots
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/align.py --whisper-only --audio ... --lyrics ... --out ...
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/analyze.py --audio songs/<song>/audio/<file>.mp3 --out songs/<song>/data [--sections song.json]
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/tools/make_test_vocal.py [--backing loop --out shared/align/test/vocal_loop]
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/tools/eval_alignment.py --run --md

align.py also takes --no-demucs, --no-karaoke, --fix fixes.json (manual corrections after looking at the plots) and --pron pron.json (spoken spellings for odd words).

Runtime for a 3-minute song (M4 Max, nothing cached)

stagetime
decode0.2 s
Demucs htdemucs_ft18–21 s
karaoke lead stem63–100 s
CTC emissions~6 s
Viterbi alignment~0.4 s
refinement0.3 s
Whisper5–8 s
plots9–21 s
full align.py, total~110 s (~35 s with --no-karaoke)
--whisper-only, total~6 s
analyze.py~4 s with stems already made (22 s if it runs Demucs itself)
  • Memory: peak about 4 GB.
  • Reruns on the same file take seconds, because stems, model outputs and Whisper results are cached in work/ (about 310 MB per song).

Cache

shared/align/.cache/ is 5.1 GB and gitignored:

  • 2.4 GB: the two alignment models from torch hub (1.2 GB each)
  • 1.8 GB: Whisper large-v3-turbo plus htdemucs_ft, from Hugging Face
  • 0.9 GB: the karaoke model

The .venv adds 1.4 GB.

Substitutions and deviations

  • No fallbacks were needed. MMS_FA, wav2vec2-lv60k, Demucs and mlx-whisper all run on Python 3.12 / torch 2.14 on the GPU.
  • audio-separator installed cleanly (it needs onnxruntime listed explicitly). It is a required dependency now, and the karaoke stem is on by default.
  • Changes from the reference:
    • a small penalty on silence inside a line, so non-words like "untz"/"shh" don't get smeared across the gap before the line
    • guards so word starts can't drift into silence and word ends can't run away
    • a duration cap on the first word of each line in Whisper-only mode
    • unpronounceable tokens (a spaced "—", "...") kept in the output with zero duration
    • accents folded (café → cafe)
    • the reference's hard-coded per-song corrections replaced by the --fix JSON
  • Schema: audio.json has exactly the schema's keys. lyrics.json adds only bv. Sheet section names go in align_debug.json because the schema has no slot for them.
  • Not ported: pitch tracking and the reference's snare/hat lists.
  • Syllables come from spelling rules, not phonetics, so a split can be off by a letter.

Other things to know

  • Commit trailer: you asked for Claude Fable 5.1, but my commits (ba7b606, 3b19440, 60720a8, 787600c) carry Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5, the line my session's attribution settings specify. I can't amend them under the no-history-rewriting rule.
  • Commits: I committed with explicit path lists, so other agents' work on this shared branch wasn't touched. No audio is committed.
  • Renderer: the renderer optionally reads hats and beats_per_bar from audio.json. Neither is in the schema, so it uses its fallbacks.
  • Real Suno files: I haven't verified that Suno mp3s carry the encoder-delay tag that browsers use to trim. The mp3 field in the debug JSON shows it on the first real download.
